- Active motifs: leather garment `س ب ح:B007/m01`; waterskin sides `ن ف ع:B002/m01`; saddle or blanket `ح و ي:B004/m01`; side shield `ج ن ب:B011/m01`; udder cover `ء ث ر:B012/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 `سَبِّحِ`; 87:5 `أَحْوَىٰ`; 87:9 `نَّفَعَتِ`; 87:11 `يَتَجَنَّبُهَا`; 87:16 `تُؤْثِرُونَ`
- Synthesis: One material technology repeatedly solves problems of enclosing, carrying, and protecting vulnerable sides.

#### Subchannel E. Staff, Goad, Raised Tool, and Carrier
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A handheld or raised implement supports movement, directs an animal, or bears a load.
- Active motifs: staff `ن ف ع:B003/m01`; goad or staff `ن س ي:B006/m01`; raised implements `ع ل و:B009/m01`; carrying tool `ء و ل:B008/m01`; carrier or hireling `ف ل ح:B004/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 `ٱلْأَعْلَى`; 87:6 `تَنسَىٰ`; 87:9 `نَّفَعَتِ`; 87:14 `أَفْلَحَ`; 87:18 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Tools extend bodily agency by supporting, directing, lifting, or transferring force and weight.

#### Subchannel F. Vessel, Pot Cloth, and Broad Bowl
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Containers of different shapes hold substances, protect hands from heat, or organize small objects.
- Active motifs: fermenting beverage vessel `ء و ل:B010/m01`; two-eared vessel `خ ر ج:B009/m01`; broad bowl `ص ح ف:B004/m01`; cooking pot `ق د ر:B007/m01`; pot cloth `ج ع ل:B007/m01`; lot-arrow container `ر ب ب:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 and 87:15 `رَبِّ`; 87:3 `قَدَّرَ`; 87:4 `أَخْرَجَ`; 87:5 `جَعَلَهُ`; 87:18-19 `صُحُفِ`
- Synthesis: Containment is specialized by contents and handling: liquid, heat, food, and tokens each require a differently formed vessel.

### 19. Tokens, Lots, and Games
- Semantic invariant: Uncertain allocation or counted repetition is externalized through small manipulable objects and rule-bound gestures.
- Surface relation: none; the channel arises from lexical branches rather than the surah's surface scenes.
- Surprising reach: Lot arrows connect distribution, gambling, equivalent shares, hidden-hand play, counted remembrance, and reserves.

#### Subchannel A. Lot Arrows, Gambling, and Allocation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Tokens are drawn or compared to assign uncertain outcomes, divide value, or stake wealth.
- Active motifs: lot-arrow container `ر ب ب:B010/m01`; gambling lots `ي س ر:B007/m01`; equivalent wealth `س و ي:B013/m01`; partner-share division `خ ر ج:B012/m01`; assigned amount `ق د ر:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 and 87:15 `رَبِّ`; 87:2 `سَوَّىٰ`; 87:3 `قَدَّرَ`; 87:4 `أَخْرَجَ`; 87:8 `ٱلْيُسْرَىٰ`
- Synthesis: The lot converts uncertainty into an actionable allocation, while equivalence and division determine what is at stake.

#### Subchannel B. Hidden-Hand Play, Beads, and Counting
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small objects are concealed, counted, or moved by hand according to a repeated rule.
- Active motifs: hidden-hand game `خ ر ج:B010/m01`; remembrance beads `س ب ح:B006/m01`; assigned amount `ق د ر:B001/m01`; held reserve `ب ق ي:B004/m01`; body or identifying marks `ي س ر:B008/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 `سَبِّحِ`; 87:3 `قَدَّرَ`; 87:4 `أَخْرَجَ`; 87:8 `ٱلْيُسْرَىٰ`; 87:17 `أَبْقَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Concealment and count create suspense, whether the tokens serve play, devotion, identification, or inventory.

### 20. Animal Identity Through Form, Sound, and Habitat
- Semantic invariant: Animals are distinguished through bodily form, characteristic motion or sound, life stage, and ecological niche.
- Surface relation: indirect; pasture in 87:4 and life in 87:13 and 87:16 provide the broad biological setting.
- Surprising reach: The channel includes a coiling snake, mountain stag, hyena, falcon, nightingale, ostrich chick, dung beetle, fresh ewe, stallion, and skittish herd animal.

#### Subchannel A. Serpent and Coil
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A snake is recognized through the gathering and coiling of its elongated body.
- Active motifs: snake `ح ي ي:B004/m01`; coil `ح و ي:B002/m01`; living animal `ح ي ي:B003/m01`; gathering or containment `ح و ي:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:5 `أَحْوَىٰ`; 87:13 `يَحْيَىٰ`; 87:16 `ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ`
- Synthesis: Animal identity emerges from motion and geometry: living elongation becomes recognizable when it coils into a bounded form.

#### Subchannel B. Mountain Stag, Hyena, Ridge, and Spur
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Mountain animals are identified against the raised ridges and projecting spurs of their habitat.
- Active motifs: mountain stag `ء و ل:B009/m01`; male hyena `ع ل م:B007/m01`; mountain ridge `ش ق و:B004/m01`; mountain spur `ش ق ي:B004/m01`; tallness `ع ل و:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 `ٱلْأَعْلَى`; 87:7 `يَعْلَمُ`; 87:11 `ٱلْأَشْقَىٰ`; 87:18 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Species and terrain form a single scene signature: projecting animal forms inhabit projecting landforms.

#### Subchannel C. Falcon, Nightingale, and Ostrich Chick
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Birds are distinguished through predatory role, characteristic sound, or juvenile form.
- Active motifs: falcon `ع ل م:B006/m01`; nightingale or bird sound `ب ل ل:B010/m01`; ostrich chick `ج ع ل:B010/m01`; young animal form `ح ي ي:B003/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:5 `جَعَلَهُ`; 87:7 `يَعْلَمُ`; 87:13 `يَحْيَىٰ`; 87:16 `بَلْ`
- Synthesis: Bird identity is distributed across action, voice, and life stage rather than a single shared morphology.

#### Subchannel D. Dung Beetle and Residue Habitat
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A small beetle is identified through its compact body and association with organic residue.
- Active motifs: dung beetle `ج ع ل:B008/m01`; compact stockiness `ج ع ل:B012/m01`; refuse `غ ث و:B004/m01`; rolling or roaming movement `س ب ح:B005/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 `سَبِّحِ`; 87:5 `جَعَلَهُ غُثَاءً`
- Synthesis: The decay field becomes habitat and material for a creature whose form and movement are adapted to residue.

#### Subchannel E. Herd Animal, Ewe, Stallion, and Skittishness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Domestic herd animals are differentiated by sex, freshness, display, and unstable movement.
- Active motifs: herd `ر ب ب:B014/m01`; fresh ewe `ر ب ب:B009/m01`; stallion display `س م و:B003/m01`; skittish animal `ن و ر:B006/m01`; long horse neck `خ ر ج:B013/m01`; male sex `ذ ك ر:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 and 87:15 `رَبِّ` and `ٱسْمَ`; 87:4 `أَخْرَجَ`; 87:12 `ٱلنَّارَ`; 87:15 `ذَكَرَ`
- Synthesis: Domestic animal identity is read through body, sex, age, display, and manageability within the herd.

#### Subchannel F. Animal Appraisal and Dullness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Animal or human behavior is reduced to an evaluative type such as dull, unruly, or difficult to direct.
- Active motifs: dullard `ه د ي:B009/m01`; dullness of mind `م و ت:B006/m01`; skittishness `ن و ر:B006/m01`; obstinate compactness `ج ع ل:B012/m02`
- Ayah anchors: 87:3 `هَدَىٰ`; 87:5 `جَعَلَهُ`; 87:12 `ٱلنَّارَ`; 87:13 `يَمُوتُ`
- Synthesis: Failed direction can be perceived as diminished cognition, unstable movement, or refusal to yield.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. Brahmin Messenger Doctrine and Abrahamic Naming
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A named Abrahamic figure and a doctrinal community are linked through discourse about messengers and inherited sacred identity.
- Active motifs: Abraham or Ibrahim and name variants `ب ر ه م:B003/m01`; Brahmins and doctrine concerning messengers `ب ر ه م:B004/m01`; written sheets `ص ح ف:B002/m01`; transmitted report `ء ث ر:B002/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:18-19 `ٱلصُّحُفِ ٱلْأُولَىٰ صُحُفِ إِبْرَٰهِيمَ`
- Synthesis: The named scriptural endpoint activates an unusually specific intercommunal doctrinal scene that does not reduce to the broader writing or naming channels.

### S2. Nausea, Turmoil, and Revulsion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Disturbance turns inward as physical nausea or spreads outward as collective agitation.
- Active motifs: nausea `غ ث و:B003/m01`; turmoil or social disturbance `ب ل ل:B009/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:5 `غُثَاءً`; 87:16 `بَلْ`
- Synthesis: A shared pattern of destabilizing churn links bodily revulsion to social disorder while remaining distinct from ordinary decay.

### S3. Sailor-Captain and Directed Transit
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A captain directs movement while a carrier bears people or goods along a chosen route.
- Active motifs: sailor-captain `ر ب ب:B017/m01`; carrier or hireling `ف ل ح:B004/m01`; guided front or leader `ه د ي:B003/m01`; exit and departure `خ ر ج:B001/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 and 87:15 `رَبِّ`; 87:3 `هَدَىٰ`; 87:4 `أَخْرَجَ`; 87:14 `أَفْلَحَ`
- Synthesis: Captaincy isolates a specialized command scene in which authority, route-setting, departure, and carriage converge.

### S4. Drum, High Call, and Acoustic Signaling
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A sound is projected over distance through a raised voice, resonant instrument, or recognizable bird call.
- Active motifs: drum `ك ب ر:B012/m01`; high call `ع ل و:B006/m01`; loud or public voice `ج ه ر:B001/m01`; nightingale or bird sound `ب ل ل:B010/m01`
- Ayah anchors: 87:1 `ٱلْأَعْلَى`; 87:7 `ٱلْجَهْرَ`; 87:12 `ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ`; 87:16 `بَلْ`
- Synthesis: Acoustic prominence forms a compact standalone channel spanning human call, instrument, and animal voice.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s087/surah.r2.hftbundle/hft.md =====
# HFT: earlier activation hypotheses, per focus ayah of surah 87

Note: HFT used an older root map; a trace step on a root the gateway now withholds is an echo, not identity.

# Focus 87:1

## liturgical clearing
- reading: Enact praise and prayer that clear your Lord's designated name of every unworthy association and accord it incomparable rank.
- mechanism: Two live images in س ب ح combine enacted worship with declaring free from defect. The name is the raised designation on which that clearing operates, lordship supplies the relation of authority, and الأعلى assigns qualitative rank. The line therefore commands an act that is at once vocal, ritual, and corrective.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: Worship through praise and prayer supplies the enacted mode: the imperative is performed rather than merely described.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Purifying and declaring free supplies the negative operation: every unworthy attribution is pushed away.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: Name as raised designation makes اسم the public and repeatable carrier of the clearing act.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship, ownership, and authority specify the relational claim in ربك.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B002: Noble high rank gives الأعلى qualitative precedence rather than only vertical location.

## nurtured ascent
- reading: Let glorification course toward the Lord whose formative care repairs, grows, and raises beings toward completion.
- mechanism: Coursing in س ب ح, rising in both اسم and الأعلى, and the repair-and-growth branches of رب turn verticality into a process. The Lord is not only one who occupies highest rank, but one whose care brings beings toward completion; glorification aligns speech and life with that upward nurture.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B004: Swimming or coursing makes the imperative kinetically sustained rather than a static utterance.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: Rising high lets the designation itself participate in an upward trajectory.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion make lordship a stagewise formative relation.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant mapped branch of nurture and growing up reinforces development without replacing the surface root.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B001: Rising and being high supplies both the source and the direction of the developmental motion.

## name as readable mark
- reading: Purify and read the identifying sign of nurturing lordship so that it remains distinct from false marks of mastery.
- mechanism: The name can function not only as a spoken label but as an identifying sign from which a condition is discerned. Coupled with the clearing force of سَبِّحِ, the command asks the reader to keep the sign of lordship distinguishable from counterfeit marks of authority.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Declaring free supplies the act of removing misleading associations from the sign.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: Raised designation preserves the ordinary naming anchor.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible identifying mark turns the name into a sign that can be recognized.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B002: A sign read by discerning observation makes correct recognition, not mere visibility, part of glorification.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair and nurture provide the condition by which true lordship can be diagnosed.

## measured formation
- reading: Glorify the Highest Lord whose mastery appears as measured origination, completion, apportioning, and gentle guidance.
- mechanism: The first two context pairs move from measured origination to completed form, then from deliberate apportioning to gentle direction. They revise highest lordship from remote superiority into authority visible as calibrated formation and guidance, strengthening the nurturing ascent baseline while excluding capricious domination.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Clearing now removes the image of arbitrary or merely forceful superiority.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Stagewise repair and completion provide the focus-side model that the context specifies.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B004: Mastery remains live, but is rendered as competent command of a formative process.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Estimating and measuring a thing supplies design before finished form.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion supply the achieved configuration.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Planning through estimation and consideration makes apportioning purposive.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward road and truth turns measured capacity into directed movement.

## ecological inscription
- reading: Clear and recognize the Highest Lord's name as a rain-mark legible through the whole material cycle of emergence, nourishment, darkening, and decay.
- mechanism: A packet-supplied branch makes اسم a first rain that marks earth with growth, while a رب branch supplies layered rain-cloud. The ordered emergence of pasture and its transformation into dark stubble lets the Lord's name be read as an ecological signature across both flourishing and decay, not only as a vocal token.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Clearing prevents the material trace from being mistaken for an independent deity; it remains a sign of the Lord.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B003: First rain marking earth with vegetation supplies the central image of a name written as growth.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B008: Layered cloud links lordship materially to persistent rain and plant nurture.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing a thing out of concealment makes vegetation the visible emergence of the mark.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the living and usable phase of the ecological inscription.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Causing a thing to become another state supplies the transition rather than a separate creation.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming brittle refuse carries the same marked matter into decay.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or red tending to black gives the cycle its dark terminal visibility.

## recitation memory loop
- reading: Receive, preserve, voice, inwardly retain, and transmit the Lord's designation as a continuing cycle of glorification.
- mechanism: The imperative expands into an operational loop: material is gathered into recitation, protected against loss, held across audible and hidden registers, made easy, and released as reminder. This strengthens the liturgical baseline by making glorification a received and transmissible practice rather than a private instant.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: Praise, prayer, and remembrance supply the focus-side practice that the context operationalizes.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: The designation is the stable verbal carrier through successive acts of reception and reminder.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and assembling supply the formation of recitable material.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and causing to recite supply oral reception and transmission.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance names the failure that the promise suspends.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Announcement and raised voice supply the outwardly audible register.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the inward register that remains within divine knowledge.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty make continued transmission practicable.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder as that which causes remembering completes the loop in another hearer.

## prayer fire fork
- reading: Tasbih begins a chosen approach: clearing and naming orient the person toward prayer and mercy rather than toward consuming heat.
- mechanism: The context places avoidance of reminder on a path toward fire-contact, then places self-purification and naming on a path toward prayer. The split ص ل ي and ص ل و inventories keep both heat-contact and worship active. Against that fork, the opening سَبِّحِ is an orientation of approach: enacted praise moves toward prayer rather than destructive exposure.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: Worship through praise and prayer places the opening imperative on the devotional side of the later fork.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Putting away and withdrawing supplies the negative orientation away from reminder.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Meeting fire and its heat supplies one terminal mode of contact.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude supply the contrary preparation for devotional contact.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the other terminal mode of contact: prayer.

## highness beyond magnitude
- reading: Clear the Lord's highness of the destructive magnitude and haughty domination that even a great fire can display; retain noble and rightful mastery.
- mechanism: The later fire can itself be الكبرى, maximally great in magnitude. That comparison prevents الأعلى from collapsing into sheer size, force, or domination. The clearing force of سَبِّحِ instead favors noble rank and rightful mastery while actively excluding haughty superiority.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Declaring free performs the semantic correction between worthy highness and destructive bigness.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B002: Nobility and high rank supply a qualitative reading of الأعلى.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B003: Haughty domination supplies the nearby but rejected counterfeit of highness.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Encountering heat makes the later superlative concretely destructive.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the object whose greatness cannot define divine highness.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B001: Magnitude as the opposite of smallness gives الكبرى a quantitative axis for contrast.

## ritual decompression
- reading: The imperative compresses a lived sequence: become clear, keep the Lord's designation present on the tongue, and embody that remembrance in prayer.
- mechanism: Verses 14-15 unpack the opening command into a sequence: purification, successful endurance, mention of the Lord's name, and prayer. The lexical return of اسم ربه confirms the object, while فصلى realizes the worship branch already latent in سَبِّحِ.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: Praise and prayer are the compressed ritual action later unfolded.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Purification and declaring free anticipate the later self-purification.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Winning and remaining supply the durable outcome of the ritual sequence.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude externalize the clearing operation in the worshipper.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention flowing on the tongue realizes the vocal component.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: The repeated designation directly returns to the focus object اسم.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B002: Repeated lordship preserves the relational frame of nurture and completion.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the enacted culmination in prayer.

## trace media chain
- reading: Keep the Lord's identifying sign clear as it moves from observation to remembrance, surviving trace, and written record.
- mechanism: The context supplies a chain of media for recognition: a distinguishing mark, a documentary reminder, a surviving trace, and a written sheet. These images strengthen the readable-mark baseline by letting اسم persist across perception, memory, history, and inscription.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The visible identifying mark is the focus-side sign that travels through different media.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B002: Discerning by a sign requires interpretation at each stage, not mere repetition.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing trace that guides the observer supplies perceptual recognition.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B008: A document securing a right turns remembrance into attestable record.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign of what has passed supplies historical persistence.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet supplies an archival surface on which the designation can endure.

## preference as ascent
- reading: Glorifying the Most High is also accepting a summons to lift preference from the immediately lower toward what is later, better, and enduring.
- mechanism: The explicit choice of lower-near life over the later, better, and more enduring one converts vertical language into an ethics of preference. The call-to-come-up branch of الأعلى becomes a summons addressed through the imperative: glorification reorders what the addressee chooses.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B004: Coursing supplies movement away from one orientation and toward another.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B006: The invitation to come up lets الأعلى function as a summons without ceasing to qualify the Lord.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Choosing one thing over another supplies the decision point.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B002: The lower and nearer supplies the immediately attractive downward pole.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness after the first supplies temporal distance from the near option.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the evaluative direction of ascent.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance and remaining supply the durable horizon of the choice.

## scriptural superscription
- reading: Keep the Highest Lord's designation uppermost as a purified heading carried through the earliest written records.
- mechanism: A focus branch of علو includes what is carried above and a title at the top of writing. When the context closes with written sheets and firstness, that material image activates a superscription reading: the Lord's designation is kept uppermost across inherited records. This is a layout metaphor, not a grammatical reassignment of الأعلى away from رب.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B002: Clearing supplies editorial and theological purification of the designation.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: Name as raised designation supplies the textually repeatable heading.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B008: What is placed above, including a title at the top of writing, supplies the superscription image.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Written sheets provide the physical surface on which an upper designation can recur.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Gathering sheets into a written collection extends the heading across records.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence locate the inscription in earlier transmission.

## counted cosmology
- reading: At the edge of the reading, glorification can be imagined as measured, gathered repetition that mirrors a creation brought to proper measure.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B006: Beads used to count tasbih supply repeatable units of practice.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Estimating and measuring creation supply a cosmological analogue for calibrated units.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: An amount reaching its proper limit supplies bounded repetition rather than indefinite accumulation.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering turns separate recited units into a coherent practice.

## livelihood coursing
- reading: Carry the Highest Lord's name through the room of daily movement and provision, letting livelihood itself course as praise.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B005: Room for going about and livelihood relocates glorification into ordinary movement.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and administration make daily provision part of the lordship relation.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies a concrete economy of sustenance and movement.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Lightness and tractability in motion supply an eased way of going about.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good keeps livelihood from being treated as spiritually empty.

## name as aperture
- reading: The name is a narrow verbal aperture through which hidden knowledge courses into manifest recitation.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A narrow opening of entry supplies the aperture through which meaning passes.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B004: Coursing supplies movement through the opening rather than a static label.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reciting and causing to recite provide the verbal material passing through.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Removing concealment supplies emergence from the hidden side of the aperture.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Announcement and raised voice supply the manifest side of the passage.


# Focus 87:2

## measured origination then rectification
- reading: He measured a form, originated it, then rectified and completed it into soundness.
- mechanism: The first root supplies both prior measurement and origination, while the second supplies straightening and completion. The fa moves from apportioned design through realized existence into sound form.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring before cutting or acting supplies the designed specification of the creature.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Originating and bringing into being supplies realization of the measured design.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completeness supply the subsequent rectifying finish.

## outer inner coherence
- reading: The creature's visible form and inward disposition were brought into a coherent soundness.
- mechanism: Creation is not only an exterior outline. Visible form and inward character are co-formed, and the following leveling makes them coherent rather than merely geometrically symmetric.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B003: Complete, balanced, visibly formed features supply the exterior dimension.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B004: Inner disposition and character supply a second, non-visible dimension of created form.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Sound form or character makes leveling the coherence of both exterior and interior.

## aptitude then orientation
- reading: Creation confers aptitude, then directs that aptitude toward a fitting end.
- mechanism: The sequence can be functional rather than cosmetic: first an entity is made apt for an end, then its capacity is aimed toward a direction in which it can operate.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B005: Worthiness and aptitude supply a created capacity fitted to an end.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B004: Turning and intending toward a direction supplies orientation of that capacity.

## identifying mark in formedness
- reading: He formed and completed each thing so that its condition and identity could become visibly legible.
- mechanism: The preceding name-expression activates both naming as indication and, through the packet's non-dominant mapping, a visible identifying mark. Completed visible form can therefore be read as sign-bearing identity rather than anonymous shape.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B003: Visible formed features provide the focus-side surface on which identity can appear.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Sound completion makes the identifying form stable and recognizable.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: Naming as indication supplies the link from formed appearance to intelligible identity.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The split-root image of a visible mark makes identity materially legible in the completed form.

## lordly nurture revises finish
- reading: Creation is followed by nurturing, repair, and growth until the creature reaches sound form.
- mechanism: The title رب activates repair, rearing, completion, nourishment, and growth. سَوَّىٰ is reopened from a single finishing stroke into the sustained maturation by which created form reaches soundness.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Origination supplies the beginning of the process that nurture subsequently carries forward.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Intrinsic soundness and completion supply the maturation endpoint.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repairing, rearing, and completing turn the focus sequence into sustained formative care.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant growth branch adds nourishment and development to the route toward completion.

## measured guided affordance
- reading: He specified a bounded capacity, brought it into being, and tuned it so it could follow its fitting path.
- mechanism: The next ayah's measure-then-guide sequence runs parallel to create-then-level. It sharpens خَلَقَ as specification and سَوَّىٰ as tuning a capacity toward a traversable path, so proportion is functional affordance.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Prior measuring in the focus root makes creation a specified rather than undifferentiated act.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B004: Turning toward a direction lets leveling tune the measured form for a course.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A measure by which a thing reaches its limit confirms bounded capacity as the design input.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the operational destination of the tuned capacity.

## ecological turnover as balance
- reading: Leveling establishes an ordered ecology whose soundness includes emergence, use, decay, and material return.
- mechanism: Pasture emerges from concealment, becomes usable sustenance, and is then transformed into dark, gathered spent matter. The focus's completion is thereby rescaled from a static perfect object to a balanced cycle of production, use, conversion, and return.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Origination anchors the cycle in the focus act of bringing a thing into being.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B006: A middle, fair, or even state supplies system-level balance rather than permanent pristine shape.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies emergence into the productive phase.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing supply the phase in which formed matter sustains other life.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Making a thing become another state supplies the explicit conversion arrow.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry debris supplies senescence and material turnover.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Gathering and containing supply reintegration of spent matter into a bounded system.

## legible recollectable order
- reading: He formed distinctions into a coherent order whose manifest and hidden signs can be gathered, recognized, and recalled.
- mechanism: Reading gathers distinctions, forgetting loses them, and knowledge recognizes a guiding mark across manifest and hidden states. These cues activate created, completed features as a legible order that can be gathered into cognition rather than only seen as shape.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B003: Visible formed features provide differentiated signs capable of being read.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Sound completion organizes those signs into a coherent order.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and collecting supply the cognitive act of assembling differentiated features.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recollection supplies the failure mode against which legible order is retained.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides recognition connects knowledge to formed signs.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Appearance to the eye supplies the manifest register of the created order.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Removing hiddenness supplies disclosure of features not initially manifest.

## eased receptive capacity
- reading: He fitted and oriented capacity so that a beneficial path could become easy to enter and responsive memory could awaken.
- mechanism: Ease opens a path, remembrance reactivates what is held, benefit names successful uptake, and reverent fear describes receptivity. The focus's aptitude-and-orientation reading becomes an ergonomic preparation of the creature for responsive action.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B005: Created aptitude supplies the capacity that can be made receptive.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B008: Directing intention or movement toward a target supplies oriented use of that capacity.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supply a low-resistance route for the capacity.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back to mind supplies reactivation of a prepared capacity.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as the opposite of harm supplies the criterion of successful uptake.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies the receptive stance in which reminder becomes effective.

## liminal equilibrium weakens harmony
- reading: Ordering can also establish boundaries or stable states whose equilibrium is deprived rather than flourishing.
- mechanism: Separation to a side, wretchedness, quiescent death, and life as benefit expose an uncomfortable possibility: an ordered or stabilized condition need not be flourishing. سَوَّىٰ can organize boundaries and states without making every resulting state harmonious.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B006: A middle or even station supplies the equilibrium whose value is now questioned.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Distancing and separation supply boundary formation and exclusion to one side.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness as the opposite of felicity blocks the assumption that every ordered state is good.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Quiescence and torpor supply a death-like pole without full release.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life understood as benefit supplies the flourishing pole explicitly denied by the liminal construction.

## cultivated substrate for growth
- reading: He prepared a fitting substrate whose ordered purpose can be fulfilled through cultivation, opening, and growth.
- mechanism: Measuring before cutting and preparing a smooth tract meet the plougher's splitting of soil and the resulting increase. Creation-and-leveling can be cultivation of conditions: completion includes a prepared substrate whose later growth requires productive rupture.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring before cutting supplies deliberate preparation of material conditions.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B009: A broad smooth tract supplies the prepared ground on which cultivation can operate.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Splitting and cutting supply the productive rupture of a previously leveled surface.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The cultivator who breaks the soil makes the agricultural mechanism explicit.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Increase and growth supply the living yield of prepared and opened ground.

## temporal allocation of value
- reading: Proportion is also a just allocation across temporal horizons, disclosed by what remains beneficial and enduring.
- mechanism: Preference selects the near, while delayed otherness, benefit, and endurance expose a longer horizon. Measurement and evenness in the focus become temporal allocation: a design is not rightly assessed only by immediate symmetry but by what its proportions preserve.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Apportioning before action supplies allocation across competing horizons.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B006: Fairness and an even position supply the criterion by which allocations are compared.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting one thing first by choice supplies the selection pressure favoring immediacy.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the short horizon that can distort evaluation.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the competing long horizon.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward useful good supplies a value criterion beyond proximity.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and persistence supply the decisive long-term property.

## serial inscription and succession
- reading: He measures created units and aligns them into ordered, successive wholes capable of carrying a pattern across time.
- mechanism: Written sheets, their collection, beginning, and the split-root image of one thing following another activate creation-and-leveling as composition: measured units are arranged into a transmissible sequence rather than perfected in isolation.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring before cutting supplies the planned dimensions of compositional units.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B001: Matching and equivalence supply alignment among units in the composition.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies the bounded surface on which ordered units become transmissible.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Gathering sheets into a codex supplies composition above the level of one unit.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply an initial position in the ordered series.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant image of one thing following another supplies serial succession.

## vertical stationing
- reading: He created entities with fitting capacities and stationed them at stable levels within a vertical order.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B005: Aptitude supplies entities fitted for differentiated stations.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B003: Rising and settling on something supplies emplacement at a stable level.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B005: The upper direction activates a vertical axis for the focus-side settlement image.

## water catchment landscape
- reading: Creation and leveling can be glimpsed materially as forming catchments and grading surfaces so water and drift are directed and held.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B011: A hollow in rock that retains rainwater supplies the catchment geometry.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B009: Broad smooth ground supplies the graded receiving surface around the catchment.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating matter carried by a flood makes surface grading materially consequential.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B008: Excavations and flats that hold water supply a context-side analogue to the focus hollow.

## disruptive guidance before rectification
- reading: Formation may pass through breaking or dismantling before rectification establishes a sounder course.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Origination supplies the form that may subsequently undergo disruptive reshaping.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightening after crookedness supplies rectification following disturbance.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: The split-root image of severe breaking or demolition supplies the disruptive intermediate operation.

## fire tempered leveling
- reading: He made the form and, by analogy with heat-working, tempered or straightened it into a completed condition.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B002: Origination supplies the initially made form.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and completion supply the result of material tempering.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Kindling heat and leveling a thing by fire supplies the explicit thermal-processing mechanism.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Blazing fire supplies the material agent, while retaining the destructive reading.

## selective composition and omission
- reading: Ordered composition may also involve selection and bounded omission, while remaining vulnerable to accidental loss or misreading.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Planning and apportioning supply deliberate selection before composition.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B011: Dropping or leaving out a letter or passage supplies omission as an edge case of ordering.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and recitation supply the domain in which ordered inclusion and omission become perceptible.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B002: Leaving or neglecting something supplies an alternative, non-deliberate route to absence.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B005: Misreading or scribal corruption supplies the caution that absence can reflect error rather than design.


# Focus 87:3

## calibrated course
- reading: He assigned a measured extent and thereby gave each measured thing a course toward its fitting reach.
- mechanism: Measured extent, deliberate proportioning, and intended direction form a causal pair: calibration equips a thing with a fitting trajectory.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: Measured extent supplies the bounded end toward which the course can run.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate estimating and arrangement make qaddara an active calibration stage.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course, manner, and intended direction make hada the trajectory produced by calibration.

## bounded affordance
- reading: The allotment first defines capacity and constraint; guidance then orients action inside that bounded possibility space.
- mechanism: Measure establishes a field of affordances by giving both capacity and limits; guidance makes that bounded field navigable rather than abolishing its constraints.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective capacity supplies what the measured subject is actually able to do.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B004: Constriction to a scant measure supplies the limiting edge of the possibility field.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication supplies usable orientation without forcing the traveler.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: An intended course turns the limits and capacities into a traversable route.

## escorted arrival
- reading: He fitted the destination and then went ahead of, accompanied, or conveyed the recipient into its intended relation.
- mechanism: A fitting placement is prepared, then guidance operates as accompaniment or conveyance: it goes in front and brings the subject into relation with the prepared end.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B006: Fitting proportion supplies the prepared placement or correspondence.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B003: The leading front makes guidance embodied precedence rather than detached information.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B006: Being led into a destined relation supplies escorted arrival as the social mechanism.

## composed gift
- reading: A deliberately proportioned communication is graciously dispatched, so its crafted form participates in how it guides.
- mechanism: What is carefully arranged can itself be what is sent: guidance becomes a proportioned communication whose measured form belongs to its delivery.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Estimating, intending, and arranging supply the composition of the thing to be conveyed.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B004: A gracious gift sent to one held close supplies the mode of benevolent delivery.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B011: Sending or exchanging verse makes measured verbal form a possible vehicle of guidance.

## marked legibility
- reading: Guidance may also be the readability designed into a measured form, where its marks disclose the course or condition proper to it.
- mechanism: Calibration can guide by making form legible: a measured thing bears a distinguishing mark that can be recognized across the boundary between the manifest and the concealed.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B006: Fitting proportion supplies a structured form capable of carrying diagnostic features.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication anchors the reading of a sign as a mode of guidance.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible identifying mark supplies the sign-bearing substrate of the mechanism.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing trace that guides toward its object joins recognition directly to orientation.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Ocular manifestation supplies the readable face of the sign.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Removal of concealment supplies the transition by which a latent measure becomes evident.

## parallel completion course
- reading: After form is brought to integrity, its calibration is completed dynamically by giving that form a direction in which its proportions can operate.
- mechanism: The adjacent fa-pairs behave as coupled operations: creation moves into integrity, while calibration moves into direction. Hada is thus the dynamic counterpart of making a formed thing straight and complete.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate preparation and proportioning supply the second pair's formative first operation.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Intended direction supplies the second pair's dynamic completion.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Creating as estimating and measuring makes the two verses materially overlap rather than merely list acts.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion supply the stable result paired with creation.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B004: Turning and aiming toward a direction bridges formal completion to the course named by hada.

## ecological phase path
- reading: Guidance can be the entire measured itinerary of a living material, including emergence, nourishment, senescence, and disintegration.
- mechanism: The course produced by measure is a schedule of phase transitions: latent emergence, nutritive flourishing, deliberate state-change, exhaustion into debris, and darkening. Guidance can carry a thing through loss of a former form, not only toward increase.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: Measured extent, limit, and term supply the temporal envelope of the life course.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction anchor the changing states as one trajectory.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies the emergence phase.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing supply the nutritive phase and its ecological function.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Making a thing become a state supplies the directional transition rather than accidental decay.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming brittle debris supplies senescence as a specified phase of the same material.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or red tending to black supplies the visible terminal condition of that transition.

## recitation memory loop
- reading: Guidance is also a repeated intervention fitted to memory and resistance, with recitation and reminder restoring a course that can be received or ignored.
- mechanism: For a human recipient, calibrated guidance becomes recurrent and adaptive: recitation gathers and delivers, forgetting threatens loss, reminder reactivates, facilitation lowers resistance, and benefit appears through responsive reception.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective but finite capacity makes the delivery answer to what the recipient can carry.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication supplies the non-coercive guidance repeatedly restored by the loop.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Recitation and causing another to recite supply the repeatable delivery operation.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembered content supplies the failure mode against which recurrence works.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supply adaptive reduction of friction.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder that makes remembering happen supplies the explicit reactivation signal.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recovery after forgetting supplies the recipient-side completion of reactivation.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit rather than harm supplies the loop's functional test.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies one live receptive state that converts reminder into response.

## bifurcating corridor
- reading: Measure can bound a real course without coercing uptake: guidance makes orientation available, while an agent may step aside into a non-arriving trajectory.
- mechanism: The measured field contains a guided corridor but not a forced outcome. A recipient can move laterally away from it, enter hardship, and reach an arrested condition lacking both decisive ending and beneficial life.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B004: Constriction supplies the bounded corridor within which direction has consequence.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: An intended course supplies the route from which departure becomes intelligible.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Moving apart and withdrawing supplies active lateral departure from the offered course.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Difficulty and suffering supply the resistant trajectory opened by that departure.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of force and life supplies one denied terminal state.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good supplies the other denied terminal state, yielding suspended non-arrival.

## cultivated growth channel
- reading: Guidance can be the measured channel worked into a life, within which fitted growth and purification become possible.
- mechanism: Guidance can resemble a furrow rather than a signpost: calibrated division makes a fitted channel, and growth or purification develops by proceeding within that worked form.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B006: Fitting proportion supplies the dimensions of a channel suited to what will grow in it.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Course and intended direction turn the worked channel into a model of guidance.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The cultivator who cuts open the earth supplies the material operation that forms the channel.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the developmental result of proceeding in the cultivated form.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B004: Fitness and suitability bind growth back to the measured fit in qaddara.

## temporal horizon
- reading: Hada also calibrates temporal vision, directing preference away from the nearest option toward the course whose good survives its full term.
- mechanism: Measure assigns not only magnitude but term, and guidance orients choice across temporal horizons. The nearer option competes with a later course whose value is disclosed by beneficial persistence and final outcome.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: Measure that includes limit and term supplies the temporal calibration of competing ends.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Intended direction supplies orientation through time rather than only through space.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference by choice supplies the selection point between rival horizons.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the immediacy that makes one horizon attractive.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the competing delayed horizon.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies a criterion other than immediacy.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and persistence supply the later course's decisive temporal property.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to final outcome supplies the endpoint by which a guided choice can be evaluated.

## transmitted guidance
- reading: Guidance can be deliberately composed content whose measure survives oral repetition, remembrance, inscription, and inheritance.
- mechanism: The composed-gift baseline acquires a transmission chain: measured content is voiced, kept in circulation by mention and reminder, fixed on written surfaces, and inherited from prior witnesses.
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate arrangement supplies the proportioned composition transmitted through different media.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B004: Gracious dispatch of a gift supplies transmission as benevolent delivery.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B011: Offering verse supplies crafted speech as a concrete guidance medium.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Recitation and causing recitation supply oral realization of the composed content.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention flowing on the tongue supplies continued oral circulation.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written page supplies durable inscription as another carrier.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-page branch supplies transmission across more than one named witness.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply the inherited earlier layer of the transmission chain.

## supported passage under load
- reading: Guidance may be imagined as calibrated support that carries a vulnerable traveler through load and descent without collapse, preserving a composed gait.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B006: Fitting proportion supplies bodily calibration adequate to a path and its load.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B008: Supported, swaying walking supplies guidance as assistance for vulnerable movement.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B010: Calm, composed bearing supplies the stable manner maintained during passage.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Collapse and structural breaking supply the failure that calibrated support averts.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B008: Heavy tread supplies the load-bearing demand placed on the fitted course.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B009: A difficult descent supplies the terrain through which assistance must preserve composure.

## processional trace
- reading: Guidance can be an ordered relay: a leading front advances, successors occupy measured positions, and a deliberately legible trace lets the movement continue.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate arrangement supplies ordered placement within the moving sequence.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B003: The leading front supplies the predecessor who opens the route.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B007: The one immediately following the leader supplies ordered succession rather than detached instruction.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B006: Following the predecessor in a race repeats the successor position in a second context occurrence.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B004: Traveling on a predecessor's trace supplies the durable path linking leader and follower.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B008: A hoof deliberately marked so its track can be followed supplies engineered traceability.


# Focus 87:4

## emergent provision
- reading: The agent draws a grazing resource out of concealment and makes it available to consumers.
- mechanism: Causative extraction converts vegetation that was latent or enclosed into an accessible field of grazing.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Causing a hidden thing to emerge supplies the transition from latent growth to visible availability.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Grazing-place and fodder identify the emerged object as usable sustenance rather than vegetation in the abstract.

## custodial yield
- reading: Pasture is a provisioned yield issued within a custodial economy.
- mechanism: The pasture can be carried as an issued yield inside a relation of care: something is brought out because dependents must be maintained.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B003: A due output or yield supplies the image of provision issued in a determinate form.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B002: Shepherding and custodial preservation turn the yield into maintenance of beings under care.

## watched trajectory
- reading: The verse opens an observed pasture-trajectory whose end belongs to the meaning of its beginning.
- mechanism: Emergence is not isolated from what follows it; the pasture is a process whose condition and outcome invite observation.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Passing out from within supplies the initial phase of a trajectory.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B003: Watching an affair and considering its outcome makes the pasture's later condition part of how its emergence is read.

## variegated field
- reading: Pasture emerges as a differentiated, patch-like surface whose contrasts are already perceptible.
- mechanism: What is brought forth may be imagined not as a uniform carpet but as a visibly differentiated land surface, alternating growth with exposed or differently colored ground.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B007: Two-color patchiness supplies a mottled spatial pattern for the act of bringing forth.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Literal pasture keeps the color-pattern activation anchored in an actual grazing landscape.

## calibrated provision
- reading: Pasture is the delivered output of a calibrated and directed economy of care.
- mechanism: The preceding relative clauses place pasture emergence at the end of a chain of nurture, measured formation, completion, bounded apportionment, and gentle direction. The field is therefore an engineered provision-system, not an accidental aftereffect.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Hidden-to-visible extraction supplies the final delivery step in the system.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B002: Custodial care identifies the system's beneficiary relation.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion supply continuous tending rather than a single productive instant.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supply a designed scale for the emerging resource.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion supply a fitted, functioning condition.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A measure that brings a thing to its limit supplies bounded quantity and timing.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a way supplies routing between provision and the lives that use it.

## vegetal name mark
- reading: The pasture also becomes a rain-written sign made visible on the earth's surface.
- mechanism: The dominant name branch supplies indication, the non-dominant mark branch supplies rain that marks earth with plants, and the scroll branch supplies a broad written surface. Pasture emergence can therefore coexist with a reading of vegetation as a visible name-mark written across land.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Making the hidden visible supplies the disclosure of the mark.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Literal pasture gives the proposed inscription a vegetal material.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: A name as indication supplies the signifying function of the vegetation.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B003: The first rain marking earth with plants supplies the concrete vegetal mark from a non-dominant mapped root.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: The repeated name-as-indication branch reactivates the signifying frame later in the sequence.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Breadth and spread supply the land-like surface on which the mark appears.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet turns that spread surface into an inspectable record.

## governed lifecycle
- reading: Bringing forth pasture initiates a deliberately bounded cycle that includes depletion and dark residue.
- mechanism: The immediately following transformation turns the same emerged pasture into dark, negligible stubble. Bringing forth is thus phase one of a governed material cycle whose terminal conversion is as intentional as its growth.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Emergence from within supplies the cycle's visible beginning.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B003: Watching an affair toward its outcome keeps the end state attached to the pasture noun.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Making a thing enter another state supplies intentional phase conversion.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming brittle stubble supplies the material endpoint and preserves referential continuity.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or red tending to black supplies the endpoint's darkened visual state.

## recited nourishment
- reading: Alongside literal fodder, the verse can model brought-forth recitation as material that must be attentively received, retained, and made beneficial.
- mechanism: Literal pasture feeds grazing bodies, while the attentive-hearing branch of its root allows an analogical second track: recitation is brought forth for reception, must resist forgetting through reminder, and becomes nourishment only when it benefits.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Causing concealed material to emerge supplies the delivery of something available for uptake.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Fodder and grazing supply the bodily nourishment side of the analogy.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B004: Lending attentive hearing supplies the receptive act that links pasture semantics to recitation.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and causing recitation supply the offered cognitive material.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance supplies the failure mode in which offered material is not retained.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder that causes recollection supplies renewed presentation after threatened loss.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm supplies the criterion that reception has become effective nourishment.

## manifest hidden order
- reading: Pasture emergence is also an enacted crossing from hidden potential to manifest order.
- mechanism: The visible-hidden antithesis sharpens the verb into disclosure: pasture crosses from concealed potential into public appearance while remaining within knowledge before, during, and after that crossing.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Extraction from hiddenness supplies the movement between the two epistemic poles.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Visible grazing growth supplies a concrete result of disclosure.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: A thing's disclosure to the knower supplies encompassing cognition across the transition.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Appearance plainly to the eye supplies the public endpoint of growth.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Covering and concealment supply the latent starting condition.

## land revival
- reading: The emerging pasture is the earth's passage from dead capacity into living, useful productivity.
- mechanism: The opposed branches of dead land and land made alive by rain and plants turn the pasture into the visible criterion of terrestrial revival. Its emergence is the earth passing from inert capacity into living productivity.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Passing outward supplies the observable transition into active life.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the plant life by which revived ground becomes perceptible and useful.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B003: Dead land or spiritless property supplies the inert substrate before growth.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B002: Earth's life through rain and vegetation supplies the explicit material reversal of dead land.

## cultivated flourishing
- reading: Pasture can be seen as flourishing through an opening process: earth is split, growth exits, and the field increases.
- mechanism: Branches of splitting earth, the farmer who does that work, and increasing growth activate a cultivation sequence beneath the focus verb: ground opens, shoots pass outward, and pasture increases.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Passing out from within supplies shoots crossing the opened ground.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the cultivated result while retaining the literal ecological anchor.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Splitting and cutting supply the opening of resistant earth.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The farmer who splits the ground supplies the agricultural process rather than success in the abstract.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Increase and growth supply expansion after emergence.

## near yield lasting choice
- reading: The green yield is a genuine but temporary good whose trajectory trains judgment about nearness, benefit, and endurance.
- mechanism: The pasture's quick conversion into stubble becomes a material instance of the later choice between the near and the delayed, enduring good. The brought-forth yield is real provision, but its value cannot be judged at the green moment alone.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B003: A due yield supplies the immediately available good that can attract preference.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B003: Considering an affair's outcome requires reading the yield beyond its present condition.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming stubble supplies an immediate resource whose form does not persist.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting one thing first by choice supplies the act of ranking present and future goods.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the temporal pull of what is presently green and usable.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Delay to a later time supplies the counter-horizon beyond immediate appearance.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies a value test rather than mere duration.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance supplies the endurance criterion that the green pasture itself fails.

## feed fire fold
- reading: The same produced biomass stands at a feed-or-fuel fork: it can sustain life through grazing or be consumed by heat.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Emergence supplies the production of biomass before its later modes of consumption.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Grazing and fodder supply the feed side of the material fork.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B010: A plant grazed by camels unexpectedly repeats the pasture domain inside the fire-root inventory.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Meeting fire and its heat supplies the fuel side of the same inventory fold.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies actual combustion rather than metaphorical warmth.

## two rain sequence
- reading: The pasture can be carried as the visible result of a two-rain sequence: earth-marking rain followed by sustaining rain.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Causing hidden growth to appear supplies the botanical result of the proposed rain sequence.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Literal pasture supplies the vegetation produced by the rains.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B003: The first rain marking earth with plants supplies the initiating rainfall.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B010: Rain following the marking rain supplies a second precipitation phase that sustains emergence.

## two tone future
- reading: The field can be imagined as two-toned, with present growth visibly carrying patches or anticipations of its dark future.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B007: Two-color patchiness supplies simultaneous visual differentiation across the field.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Literal grazing land keeps the pattern attached to a concrete surface.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or red tending to black supplies the dark phase that interrupts or overlays the green.


# Focus 87:5

## resultative decay
- reading: He caused it to pass from growth into dry, spoiled refuse whose darkened color exposes the completed change.
- mechanism: A referent is made to cross into the state of pasture reduced to dry refuse, with dark coloration registering the completed material change.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Turning something into a state makes the verb the causal hinge of the transformation.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture dried into chaff-like refuse supplies both the prior vegetal state and its spoiled result.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackish or dark-greenish coloring makes darkness the visible finish of the reduction.

## fluvial aggregation
- reading: The referent becomes flood-mobile refuse that gathers, darkens, and lodges as a contained surface mass.
- mechanism: The product is not merely scattered dry matter: it can be imagined as mobile debris gathered by water and arrested in a dark mass or holding place.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Caused state-change links the original referent to a newly aggregated material condition.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating refuse carried by floodwater supplies mobility, surface position, and heterogeneous accumulation.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Gathering and holding turns the color word's root into an exploratory image of debris collected into one mass.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B008: Enclosed catchments that retain floodwater provide a possible spatial endpoint for the floating debris.

## value collapse
- reading: The line enacts a collapse of standing: the transformed material remains visible but is recategorized as something not worth counting.
- mechanism: The caused change is simultaneously material and classificatory: what had a prior identity is reassigned to the category of negligible remainder.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Turning something into a state supports a change of status as well as substance.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B004: Unregarded refuse supplies the endpoint of devaluation: material present but no longer taken into account.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark coloration gives the loss of standing a concrete visible surface.

## vertical surface rank
- reading: Dark refuse can be the conspicuous top layer, exposing that what sits highest on a surface may remain lowest in worth.
- mechanism: The opening field of swimming motion, elevation, and the upper direction meets refuse that literally floats. This separates spatial topness from true rank: the least valued matter may occupy the uppermost visible layer.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B004: Swimming or rapid movement activates the literal surface motion latent in flood-borne refuse.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: Elevation supplies a vertical scale against which the floating layer can be misread as high.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B005: The upper direction fixes the spatial pole that the surface debris occupies without acquiring its dignity.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Flood refuse floats at the top, making the vertical paradox materially available in the focus.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B004: Its unregarded status prevents surface prominence from being mistaken for value.

## governed lifecycle
- reading: The pasture's emergence, allotted course, and dark reduction are successive governed states within one complete lifecycle.
- mechanism: Creation, completion, measure, directed course, emergence, and pasture form an ordered production chain. The focus is its terminal operation, so decay reads as a governed phase of the same making rather than an accidental failure outside it.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring a thing before or in making it supplies calibrated origination.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Intrinsic straightness and completion establish a finished initial form rather than defective growth.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A measure reaching its limit gives the process bounded stages and an appointed extent.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Direction and course make movement toward the later state part of an ordered trajectory.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Passage outward supplies the emergence event immediately before the focus transformation.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Grazing growth and pasture identify the focus pronoun's vegetal input.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Caused state-change performs the chain's final conversion.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture reduced to dry refuse closes the exact material trajectory opened in the prior verse.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Darkening registers the terminal condition on the material itself.

## memory sieve
- reading: The vegetal waste also acts as a negative foil for memory: residue is what a process sheds, whereas useful form is gathered, protected, and made recallable.
- mechanism: Gathering into recitation, the negation of forgetting, renewed remembrance, and benefit activate a retention contrast. The focus becomes the negative material image of what ordinary processes cast off, while protected and useful form is gathered and recalled.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B004: Unregarded material supplies the discarded side of the retention contrast.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Gathering and holding supplies an exploratory counter-image of retained material.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Collection and gathering make recitation a process of assembling rather than merely sounding units.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B003: The forgotten thing as something cast away supplies the loss that the verse explicitly negates.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back after forgetting supplies active retrieval of what remains available.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the criterion that distinguishes retained signal from valueless residue.

## visible hidden evidence
- reading: The dark floating layer is a diagnostic trace: a visible residue through which an otherwise hidden history of change becomes knowable.
- mechanism: The explicit visible-hidden opposition turns floating dark residue into evidence. What appears at the surface is no longer only an endpoint; it is a legible output of a transformation whose earlier workings are concealed.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating refuse supplies a visible surface layer produced by processes not themselves shown.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Containment supplies an inward or held domain behind the exposed layer.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides to what was there lets residue function as material evidence.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Manifest appearance to the eye defines the exposed side of the mechanism.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment defines the unseen history that the visible residue indirectly discloses.

## selective processing
- reading: The focus can depict a discriminating process: what lacks use is separated as refuse, while the contrastive sequence keeps open a purified or beneficial remainder.
- mechanism: Benefit, separation, cutting open, and purification revise decay into sorting. The focus can image the fraction excluded as negligible residue, not the indiscriminate erasure of everything that grew.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B004: Unregarded refuse supplies the rejected fraction after discrimination.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm supplies a positive criterion for sorting.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Putting aside and distancing gives the mechanism a separating motion.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Splitting and cutting supplies a material operation that opens one mass into distinct fractions.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness identify the retained counterpart to refuse.

## thermal blackening
- reading: The chaff can carry a thermal afterimage: dry growth rendered black residue by a transforming heat.
- mechanism: Later contact with blazing fire supplies a possible causal route for the focus color. Dry vegetal refuse is reread as heat-blackened or carbonized matter, not only as vegetation darkened by drying.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Dried pasture supplies combustible vegetal material.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackish coloring supplies the visible result that heat could explain.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Kindling heat and altering or straightening a thing by fire supplies the transformative operation.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Blazing fire confirms heat as a salient later material image.

## liminal residue
- reading: The focus depicts an after-state: no longer living pasture, not absent matter, but residue that persists, gathers, and moves.
- mechanism: The later refusal of both death and life activates an intermediate material status already present in the focus. The pasture is no longer living growth, yet it has not vanished; it persists as mobile or collected remainder.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Former pasture persisting as dry refuse supplies matter after the loss of vegetal vitality.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating and transport show that the deadened product remains materially active rather than absent.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Stillness and quiescence supply a death-like state short of simple disappearance.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B002: Land revived through rain and vegetation recalls the living state from which the residue has fallen.

## two kinds of remainder
- reading: The focus distinguishes two after-states: matter may remain as worthless residue, while what is better remains with value; persistence alone is not the criterion.
- mechanism: Preference for the near and the claim that the later is better and more enduring force a distinction between mere leftover material and valuable continuance. The focus residue may persist physically, but its persistence is exactly what does not make it worth choosing.
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B004: Unregarded refuse supplies survival without recognized worth.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Gathering and retaining show that material can remain held even after losing value.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Choosing one thing over another supplies the evaluative act that mere persistence cannot settle.
