  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B009: A remaining old fatty trace or extracted remnant activates a second, materially specific kind of leftover.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness places immediate availability on one side of the choice.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time places delayed outcome against the near surface.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward useful good makes benefit, not mere duration, the value test.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance supplies durable remaining as the explicit positive comparison.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B002: The remainder sense makes the lexical collision with focus refuse direct while preserving their difference in value.

## material record
- reading: Without recovering practical worth, the dark residue can still be evidentiary: a surface inscription recording the passage from rain-marked growth to exhaustion.
- mechanism: A split-root rain mark, a distinguishing sign, a surviving trace, and a broad written surface converge on the focus. Vegetation first marks the land, then its dark residue remains on the surface as a material record of the cycle that produced it.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B003: The first rain marking the earth with vegetation supplies an initial inscription in living material.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: A floating layer of gathered plant residue supplies the later physical trace left on a surface.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark coloration makes the residual trace visually legible.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides to its cause lets the dark layer signify prior growth.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign of what once was turns residue into historical evidence.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Breadth and spread supply the land or water surface on which the trace can be read.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet supplies the record analogy that converts a residual layer into material script.

## visceral ecology
- reading: The same refuse can be felt from inside as a nauseating, gut-coiling encounter with corruption.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Caused state-change gives the cross-domain image a single transformation frame.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B003: A stomach or soul churning with nausea supplies visceral recoil from the product.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B003: Inward intestinal coils locate the churning inside a containing body.

## preferred surface bloom
- reading: It can also figure a near, visible surface bloom that attracts preference precisely while concealing its refuse-like substance.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Flood-borne refuse supplies the floating layer to which the later branch can attach.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark green-black coloring makes the surface layer visually compatible with an algal bloom.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B007: Algae rising on water turns preference into an exploratory image of choosing what blooms visibly at the surface.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness makes the surface bloom immediately available and therefore tempting.

## congealed end state
- reading: Alongside drying, the focus can momentarily be seen as matter concentrating and congealing at a liquid surface into a dark terminal layer.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Scum and low material gathered on liquid supply a surface phase capable of thickening.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B008: A water-holding enclosure supplies the bounded liquid environment in which matter accumulates.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B005: Liquid thickening or congealing at its final outcome supplies an alternative physical pathway to a dense residual layer.


# Focus 87:6

## causative retention
- reading: The speaker will actively make the addressee recite, and that caused transfer establishes protected recall.
- mechanism: The speaker does not merely predict the recipient's reading; the causative act installs recitation in the recipient, and the linked negation presents protected recall as its result.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and teaching another to recite supplies the externally caused vocal transmission.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recall supplies the failure that the caused recitation is presented as blocking.

## gathered sequence
- reading: Making recite gathers units into a coherent whole whose internal order is kept from dispersing.
- mechanism: Recitation gathers discrete sounds or units into an ordered whole; non-forgetting preserves not only items but their joins and recoverable sequence.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Collecting and gathering recasts recitation as assembly of dispersed units into one repeatable sequence.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recall threatens the gathered sequence with dispersal and supplies the prevented breakdown.

## nonabandonment
- reading: The bestowed recitative pattern is also a course that will not be left or neglected.
- mechanism: The causative recitation establishes a patterned course; the negation can therefore resist leaving or neglecting that course as well as involuntary memory loss.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B011: A manner, pattern, and directed course supplies recitation with an ongoing path rather than a single performance.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B002: Leaving or abandoning supplies a volitional and practical dimension to what the negation prevents.

## formed reciter
- reading: The promise begins forming a learned devotional reciter whose continuing practice resists neglect.
- mechanism: Being made to recite can form a learned, devotional agent; non-abandonment then describes continuity of a reciter's practice rather than storage of words alone.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B006: The devout or learned reader image supplies an identity and practice that can be formed through caused recitation.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B002: Leaving or neglecting supplies the discontinuity that an enduring reciter identity resists.

## fitted capacity
- reading: The speaker fits a guided recitative capacity whose designed operation includes stable recall.
- mechanism: The preceding sequence of measuring, bringing to completion, planning to a limit, and gently guiding makes caused recitation look like a fitted capacity with a guided operating path. Non-forgetting is therefore designed stabilization, not an isolated suspension of normal cognition.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Teaching another to recite supplies the focus act whose recipient-side capacity is being fitted.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recall supplies the cognitive failure that fitted guidance is inferred to stabilize.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supplies an image of capacity being set to an appropriate scale.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Intrinsic straightness and completion supplies the stabilization of the recipient rather than only of the message.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate planning by measure supplies the ordered design behind the promised retention.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies continuing guidance for the installed recitative course.

## retrieval against decay
- reading: Recitation actively retrieves and regathers a vulnerable sequence so that it does not decay into an unregarded remnant.
- mechanism: The vegetation sequence moves from hidden emergence to gathered dark refuse. Against that material cycle, recitation becomes repeated retrieval and reassembly: what is latent is brought out and gathered before it becomes an unregarded cast-off trace.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Collecting and gathering supplies the reassembly by which recitation keeps a sequence available.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B003: The forgotten cast-off thing supplies the endpoint from which the recited material is protected.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies retrieval from latency into voiced availability.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry refuse supplies a local image of once-living material losing usable organization.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Gathering, securing, and containing supplies a counterforce that keeps material from dispersing.

## selective disclosure
- reading: The addressee receives dependable but willed access, with recitation governing movement from hidden knowledge into public voice.
- mechanism: The immediate exception places retention inside will, while knowledge spans what is publicly voiced and what remains hidden. Recitation is thus a governed passage from concealed availability into audible declaration; its preservation is real but not autonomous possession.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and reciting supplies the audible disclosure whose availability is being governed.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recall supplies the bounded failure admitted by the following exception.
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B002: Will attached to a thing supplies the explicit boundary around the otherwise comprehensive promise.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: A thing becoming disclosed to a knower supplies full knowledge beyond the recipient's current recall.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Public declaration and raised voice supplies the manifest pole naturally realized by recitation.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the unmanifest pole that remains known even when not made recitably present.

## eased fluency
- reading: Retention works through an opened, compliant recitative path that makes retrieval fluent.
- mechanism: The following doubled y-s-r cue turns preservation into fluency: a patterned recitative course is opened after difficulty and made light and compliant in movement. Ease becomes a mechanism of reliable retrieval rather than a separate benefit.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B011: A patterned directed course supplies the repeatable path along which recitation can become fluent.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recall supplies the retrieval friction that an eased path overcomes.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supplies removal of obstruction from the recitative path.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Lightness and compliance in movement supplies embodied fluency rather than static storage.

## mnemonic relay
- reading: The addressee is stabilized as a mnemonic relay whose retained voice can reactivate memory in others.
- mechanism: The receiver of caused recitation becomes a reminder, and the reminder elicits recollection in another when it benefits. Preserved recitation therefore forms a social relay: reception, voiced reminder, recipient recollection, and practical uptake.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Teaching recitation supplies the first transmission link received by the addressee.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Protected recall supplies continuity needed for the recipient to become a reliable transmitter.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something to mind after forgetting supplies the intended effect in later hearers.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention running on the tongue supplies the voiced middle link between retained recitation and another's recollection.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as the opposite of harm supplies a functional test for whether the reminder completes the relay.

## lived nonneglect
- reading: Not forgetting also means not sidelining the recitation into inert knowledge without lived response.
- mechanism: The one who places the reminder to the side enters a condition denied both death's release and life's benefit. This sharpens n-s-y as neglect: not forgetting is sustained responsiveness that keeps recitation from becoming cognitively present but existentially inert.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Recitation supplies the received content whose effect can either remain active or be sidelined.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B002: Leaving and neglecting supplies the focus-level bridge from memory failure to failed response.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Putting at a distance and withdrawing supplies the enacted shape of neglecting a reminder.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Stillness and extinction-like torpor supplies one pole of an inert response.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good supplies the positive efficacy explicitly denied in the frozen condition.

## practice cycle
- reading: The recipient becomes a practitioner who repeatedly renews recitation through reform, voiced remembrance, and worship.
- mechanism: Purification, voiced remembrance, and prayer give the formed-reciter baseline an enacted cycle. The protected recitation is metabolized through reform, utterance, and worship, each pass renewing rather than merely archiving it.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B006: The devout or learned reader supplies the practitioner identity developed by the later sequence.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B002: Non-abandonment supplies continuity across repeated acts of reform, remembrance, and prayer.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectification supplies the self-forming effect of carrying recitation into practice.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention flowing on the tongue supplies renewed vocal activation of what was retained.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: The specific act of worship supplies the embodied completion of voiced remembrance.

## temporal salience
- reading: Not forgetting also keeps a delayed but durable horizon presently actionable against the pull of what is near.
- mechanism: The later contrast between choosing what is near and what comes later yet lasts activates the focus root's appointed-time image. Non-forgetting becomes resistance to temporal salience: recitation keeps the delayed and durable horizon present against the pressure of the immediate.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B005: An appointed time arriving or being delayed supplies a temporal rhythm within the act of recitation.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B002: Leaving or neglecting supplies the risk that a less immediate horizon will be dropped from action.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Giving something priority in choice supplies the selection pressure acting on what remains salient.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the immediacy that competes for attention and preference.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the horizon that memory must hold present before arrival.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and persistence supplies the lasting value obscured by the nearer option.

## transmission across media
- reading: The promise makes that recipient a living oral continuation of teaching already preserved in earlier written witnesses.
- mechanism: The ending identifies the teaching with earlier written leaves, while q-r-hamza carries both gathering and recitation. Caused oral performance becomes a living reassembly of an older written witness, and non-forgetting preserves continuity across media and generations.
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Collecting and gathering supplies the assembly of distributed teachings into a coherent recitative whole.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and reciting supplies the oral performance that reactivates written witness.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Protected recall supplies continuity when teaching moves from written leaves into a living voice.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written leaf supplies the durable graphic medium with which present recitation is aligned.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Gathering leaves into a codex supplies a material counterpart to q-r-hamza gathering a recitable whole.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supplies the earlier temporal layer carried forward by present recitation.

## gestational carrying
- reading: The addressee is made to carry a gathered recitation until it matures into voice without being prematurely cast off.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B004: A womb enclosing pregnancy supplies the contained carrying phase of the exploratory model.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B003: The forgotten cast-off thing supplies the failed outcome that protected carrying prevents.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies the later emergence of internally carried recitation into voice.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supplies maturation between reception and voiced emergence.

## oral error correction
- reading: Preserved oral recitation also acts as an error-detecting reference for written or subsequently read transmission.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and teaching recitation supplies the repeatable oral reference channel.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Protected recall supplies stability in the reference channel across repeated performances.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B005: Corruption or misreading of written form supplies the error mode that a stable oral recitation could expose.


# Focus 87:7

## volition within total knowledge
- reading: The knowledge clause constrains how the exception is heard: willing operates with no blind side across both disclosure and concealment.
- mechanism: Willing selects or brings about a knowable thing, while knowledge spans both public disclosure and concealment. The exception therefore marks a volitional boundary inside a complete epistemic field, not an item escaping knowledge.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B002: Will directed toward a thing supplies the selecting agency of the exception and may include bringing the selected thing about.
  - 87:7 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed divine name identifies the willing and knowing subject rather than leaving the exception impersonal.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: A thing becoming clear to a knower supplies comprehensive apprehension as the frame around the exception.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Open declaration and raised voice supply the publicly available pole of what is known.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Covering and secrecy supply the inaccessible-to-others pole that remains equally within knowledge.

## moving disclosure threshold
- reading: The verse also knows a moving threshold: concealment, partial glimmer, extraction, and full disclosure are all already apprehended.
- mechanism: The branch inventories make manifestation reversible and graded. Water can be cleared into appearance, a cover can be removed, and a concealed flash can glimmer without becoming fully public. Knowledge thus reaches the transitions by which something hides, leaks through, or becomes manifest.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B008: Cleaning a well until its water appears turns overt presence into the result of a material disclosure process.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Removing a cover shows that the concealment root itself can carry the reversal from hidden to exposed.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B004: A faint flash within cloud supplies an intermediate state that is neither fully concealed nor fully manifest.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Knowing as disclosure to the knower gathers every stage of the visibility gradient into one apprehension.

## sign and hidden referent
- reading: It can also name direct command of both public evidence and the concealed referents, causes, or interiors that evidence only indicates.
- mechanism: An outward mark can identify and guide toward something not itself present to view. The overt/hidden pair can therefore be read semiotically: public signs and the concealed realities to which they point are both known, without knowledge being reduced to inference from the sign.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides to a thing supplies the relation between an available sign and an absent referent.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Plain appearance to the eye supplies the publicly legible surface of the sign relation.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B002: Covers and inner things supply realities not exhausted by what their outward marks disclose.
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B001: The knowable and reportable thing gives the sign a determinate object rather than treating signs as free-floating symbols.

## emergence and decay cycle
- reading: They become stages in a governed cycle of emergence and recession: what is concealed can be brought forth, and what is manifest can be transformed into dark remainder.
- mechanism: Measured formation and guidance lead into bringing pasture out, then transforming it into dark floating residue. This activates the focus pair as phases of a governed material cycle: what was hidden emerges openly, and what flourished openly is darkened, contained, or carried away, all within informed willing.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B002: Will toward a thing anchors the context's ordered transformations back in the focus exception.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B008: Water made apparent by clearing supplies a material image for emergence into the open.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Extraction from under cover makes hiddenness a reversible phase rather than a permanent class.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning a thing supplies an ordered beginning rather than accidental appearance.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Managing by estimation and foresight supplies the governing logic of the sequence.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a path turns measured capacity into directed development.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing a thing out of concealment explicitly links the growth scene to the focus visibility threshold.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Causing a thing to become another state supplies the reversal from pasture to residue.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Debris floating on a torrent supplies the exposed remnant of a prior living state.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or red tending to black supplies the obscuring terminal phase of the visible growth.

## managed recitation memory
- reading: The exception governs recitational retention, while divine knowledge holds together the sounded text, its latent memory, and what may drop from human access without dropping from knowledge.
- mechanism: Recitation moves gathered language from latent possession into voiced articulation; forgetting removes it from ready access, while ease, reminder, and written sheets preserve routes of recovery. The exception now reads as a divinely governed limit on retention or retrieval, and the knowledge clause spans both audible output and what remains inward or unavailable.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B002: Will directed toward a thing makes the exception an active retention boundary rather than a random lapse.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Complete apprehension prevents human inaccessibility through forgetting from becoming divine inaccessibility.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B011: The voiced consonant supplies a precise phonetic form for the recitation's publicly articulated layer.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B002: Inner and physically covered things supply the latent, unuttered, or temporarily inaccessible layer.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and causing recitation provide the concrete operation immediately bounded by the exception.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss from remembrance supplies the access failure to which the exception grammatically attaches.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening into ease after difficulty supplies a facilitated route from latent possession to usable recitation.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back after forgetting supplies recovery rather than treating absence from recall as erasure.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet externalizes language into a durable public support for what memory may not presently surface.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Priority and beginning extend the recitation-memory mechanism backward into earlier textual transmission.

## divine and human selection asymmetry
- reading: It becomes selection from a horizon that includes concealed and delayed consequences, pointedly unlike preference captured by what is nearest and most apparent.
- mechanism: The later contrast between choosing the near life and the later, better, more lasting one creates two selection regimes. Human preference is pulled toward what is near and presently apparent; the focus attributes divine willing to a knower of both the apparent and concealed, so its selection is not confined by immediacy.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B002: Will toward a thing supplies the divine selection term that the later human preference sharpens by contrast.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Full apprehension gives divine selection an epistemic range unavailable to preference governed by nearness.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Plain appearance supplies the currently available pole toward which near-term preference can be biased.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies outcomes not presently available to sight but still included in informed willing.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preferring one thing by choice supplies the explicit human counterpart to willing.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the temporal and perceptual bias of the chosen present life.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the less immediately apparent alternative.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies a value criterion that can oppose mere nearness.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and persistence supply the longer horizon hidden by present preference.

## hidden receptivity to reminder
- reading: It also reads as knowledge of why one public reminder becomes beneficial recollection in one hearer and active avoidance in another.
- mechanism: A reminder is publicly uttered, but its benefit is disclosed only in divergent uptake: recollection with reverent fear or active avoidance with misery. The focus knowledge pair now supports a diagnostic reading in which the outward encounter and concealed receptivity are both known.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Apprehension of a thing supplies knowledge of the response before it is exhausted by outward behavior.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Public declaration supplies the audible reminder and the response visible in public conduct.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Secrecy and concealment supply inward receptivity or resistance not identical with public exposure.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder that causes recollection supplies the public intervention whose effects diverge.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm makes reception functional rather than merely acoustic.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies one concealed disposition that becomes visible as recollection.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Deliberate distancing and withdrawal supply the contrary outward trajectory.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposed to felicity gives the avoidance trajectory its evaluative condition.

## uttered name and concealed devotion
- reading: The name also sits inside a repeated invocation pattern, so knowledge of overt and hidden spans the sounded name and the concealed relation enacted through it.
- mechanism: The context frames the focus with glorifying the Lord's name and later remembering that name in prayer. The fixed divine name inside the focus exception becomes part of an invocation circuit: voiced naming is overt, while the devotion, care, and orientation carried by it can remain concealed.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed name used in invocation makes the focus occurrence participate in the context's repeated naming.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Raised or public utterance supplies the audible surface of glorification and prayer.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the unvoiced orientation or devotion that outward naming may carry.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: Worship through glorification and prayer starts the invocation circuit.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: A name as indication makes vocal naming a sign that points beyond its sound.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and completing supply the relational content invoked by the title Lord.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention running on the tongue supplies the later voiced return of the name.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: The specified act of worship turns naming into enacted devotion rather than bare sound.

## network of names marks traces and pages
- reading: God knows the whole sign relation: public names, marks, traces, and records, plus the realities and histories they reveal only partially.
- mechanism: Name, visible mark, surviving trace, and written page form a chain of externalizations. Each makes an absent or hidden reality publicly legible without being identical to it. This strengthens the focus-only semiotic model: divine knowledge includes signs, what produced them, and what no surviving sign exposes.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides to a thing provides the focus hinge for the context's external signs.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Plain visibility supplies the legible surface shared by mark, trace, and page.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B002: Covered interiors supply causes and referents that remain beyond their external signs.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mapped image of a visible mark that makes a thing known activates the sign mechanism inside the word for name.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining trace that indicates what once was supplies evidence whose cause is absent.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies deliberate durable inscription rather than an accidental trace.

## latency between life and death
- reading: Whatever hides may include dormant capacity, suspended process, or faintly present life that has not yet crossed into open manifestation.
- mechanism: Stillness can resemble death, earth can become alive through rain, growth can increase, and a thing can persist. Against the explicit neither-dies-nor-lives suspension, hiddenness can denote latency rather than mere secrecy, while overt presence is an activated phase. Knowledge spans dormant, active, and suspended modes.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Appearance plainly available to perception supplies the activated or manifest phase.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B004: A faint flash held within cloud supplies latent presence that has not become fully public.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Stillness and dormancy resembling sleep or decay blur the boundary between absence and active life.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B002: Earth becoming alive through rain and vegetation supplies activation from a previously dormant state.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply a process by which latent capacity becomes outwardly realized.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Persistence supplies continuity across changing degrees of manifestation.

## phonetic listening buffer
- reading: A narrower phonetic reading appears: attentive reception, an inward retention buffer, and voiced articulation are all known across the exception.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B006: Listening closely supplies an auditory shadow around the will-root form without displacing its grammatical sense of willing.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B011: The voiced consonant supplies the articulated acoustic stream that can be heard and retained.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B002: An inner covered thing supplies the silent memory buffer from which articulation emerges.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Causing recitation supplies the live speech event that activates the auditory branches.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss from remembrance supplies the possible break between inner retention and voiced output.

## route through unseen terrain
- reading: It can cast a route: propulsion, landmarks, exposed but unfamiliar ground, concealed stretches, side paths, traces, and final outcome are all under one guiding knowledge.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B003: Driving something toward a matter supplies route propulsion as an exploratory shadow of willing.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A landmark that guides to a thing supplies orientation within the route.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B009: Traversing land without prior knowledge supplies the exposed but epistemically unfamiliar terrain.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies unseen portions and consequences along the route.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction to the path supplies guidance rather than mere force.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Lightness and compliance in movement supply facilitated travel.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B005: Leading something to the side supplies the divergent avoidance route.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B004: Following a predecessor's trace supplies route continuity through evidence left behind.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an outcome supplies the route's terminal horizon.

## manifest glare
- reading: Human access can fail at both extremes, through cover or through overwhelming disclosure; divine knowing is not organized by that human visibility hierarchy.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B004: An eye overcome or dazzled by sunlight makes excessive visibility function like concealment for a human observer.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Ordinary covering supplies the opposite route to inaccessibility, allowing glare and concealment to converge in effect.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Knowledge as clear apprehension belongs to the divine subject despite both human perceptual failures.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B005: The upper direction supplies the elevated source-zone conventionally associated with overpowering light.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B001: Light and illumination activate the perceptual excess latent in the al-jahr eye branch.

## clarified water and residue
- reading: They can be imagined as outputs of separation: clarified appearance, extracted matter, floating residue, contained remainder, and later settling are all known through the process.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B008: Removing mud until well water appears supplies clarified output as the overt pole.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Extraction from beneath cover supplies the separating action between hidden material and exposed result.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment extends the extraction mechanism into the context.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating torrent debris supplies a visible residue separated from what the flow carries.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B001: Gathering and containment supply the retained material that does not simply vanish when clarification occurs.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B005: Liquid thickening and congealing at its outcome supplies a late material reversal from flow to settled remainder.


# Focus 87:8

## opening readiness
- reading: We will fit you for an opening that becomes ready after resistance.
- mechanism: The first occurrence acts on the addressee while the second names the target condition. The repetition makes ease both an imparted capacity and an opening for which that capacity is fitted.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Readiness and opening after resistance supply both the operation performed on the addressee and the condition toward which it tends.

## pliant gait
- reading: The addressed person is made supple and quick to follow the feasible course.
- mechanism: Ease can be a trained responsiveness: light, compliant movement that follows a viable course without stiffness.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Pliant, quick-following movement turns facilitation into an acquired manner of proceeding.

## spacious means
- reading: Ease means being supplied with enough range and means to meet what remains.
- mechanism: Facilitation may work by furnishing means and amplitude rather than by shrinking the demand.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B003: Prosperity and breadth of means make ease an enlargement of usable capacity.

## minimal sufficiency
- reading: The addressee is brought to a minimal yet sufficient load or interval.
- mechanism: The operation may reduce an assignment, interval, or burden to the least sufficient quantity rather than abolish it.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B002: Small amount supplies a scalar mechanism in which facilitation is achieved by limiting the required quantum.

## fluid mobility
- reading: The addressee is made able to move fluidly through a medium whose resistance still has to be negotiated.
- mechanism: The opening motion cue turns pliancy into medium-sensitive locomotion: ease is the capacity to keep moving by adjusting one's strokes to what carries or resists.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light, compliant motion anchors the changed reading in the focus root.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B004: Running or swimming contributes movement through a medium and makes pliancy function as navigational adjustment.

## marked steps
- reading: Ease is produced by articulating a course into signs the addressee can recognize and follow.
- mechanism: A visible sign can convert an otherwise vague easy course into separated, recognizable steps: facilitation works by making the route legible.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B008: Separated lines or bodily marks anchor ease as a route articulated into distinguishable traces.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The non-dominant visible-mark branch supplies the sign that makes each step recognizable.

## formed capacity
- reading: The addressee is nurtured into a widened competence adequate to the course.
- mechanism: Repair, completion, feeding, and growth make facilitation developmental. The addressee is progressively formed into adequacy rather than merely handed an easier circumstance.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B003: Breadth of means anchors the result as increased capacity.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion supply the gradual formative operation that produces capacity.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The split-root feeding and growth image sharpens formation as increase sustained over time.

## measured fit
- reading: Person, measure, and route are calibrated to one another, and their fit is what makes the opening ready.
- mechanism: Readiness, straight completion, bounded measure, and gentle guidance form a design sequence. Ease emerges from fit among the addressee's formed capacity, the assigned measure, and a guided route.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Ready opening anchors the resulting fit in the focus's ordinary ease branch.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B005: Worthiness and preparedness contribute a subject made apt for what follows.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion contribute coherent internal form.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A quantity reaching its proper limit supplies calibration rather than unlimited demand.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a route supplies direction without replacing the traveler's movement.

## ecological provision
- reading: Ease is productive viability maintained through emergence, use, depletion, and renewal.
- mechanism: Emergence, pasture, transformation, dry debris, and darkening place productive ease inside a cycle of provision and exhaustion. Viability lies in being carried through changing states, not in freezing the fertile state.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B006: Increase in milk and offspring anchors the focus as productive provision.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies the emergence phase of provision.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing connect the focus's livestock productivity to an actual sustaining field.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Causing a thing to enter another state supplies the cycle's transition.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry debris prevents productive ease from being read as permanent lushness.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Darkening supplies the visible terminal state of the ecological transition.

## retrievable recitation
- reading: The addressee is made reliably ready to gather, voice, and recover recitation without pretending that contingency has vanished.
- mechanism: Recitation, threatened loss, contingent willing, and knowledge spanning voiced and hidden states recast ease as reliable availability. The addressee is enabled to gather and retrieve utterance across changes of cognitive state.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Becoming ready anchors the focus as enabled access rather than a claim of effortless performance.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, recitation, and causing another to recite identify the material that must remain available.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance supplies the failure mode against which readiness operates.
  - 87:7 **شَآءَ** ش ي ء B002: Will attached to a thing bounds the facilitation and prevents it from becoming an autonomous guarantee.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to the knower supplies complete cognitive supervision of what is available or latent.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Public announcement and raised voice provide the expressed pole of recitation.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment provides the latent pole from which utterance may need to be retrieved.

## selective reminder channel
- reading: Ease equips a reliable reminder channel while leaving hearers able either to enter it or move themselves aside.
- mechanism: Facilitated recitation becomes a transmissive channel for restoring remembrance. Benefit and awe open reception, while deliberate side-stepping and hardship describe a competing trajectory; the speaker's enabling does not predetermine every hearer's response.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Quick, compliant following anchors the focus as readiness to carry a reminder forward.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back to presence after forgetting defines the reminder's restorative function.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit rather than harm supplies the criterion under which transmission takes effect.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies an inward posture that admits remembrance.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Withdrawal and self-distancing supply the hearer's active refusal of the available route.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Hardship and suffering name the rival trajectory reached by avoidance.

## scaled burden
- reading: Ease may be the merciful limiting of intensity or duration to a bearable, sufficient measure.
- mechanism: The small-amount branch of the focus becomes a scalar countermodel to hardship, magnitude, heaviness, and suspended vitality. Facilitation can reduce burden to a survivable quantum even when it does not erase ordeal.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B002: Littleness supplies the focus-side scale on which burden can be limited.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Severe difficulty supplies the opposing high-resistance state.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B010: Heaviness and arduousness turn the explicit superlative context into a burden scale.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of force and life marks one terminal release that the described state withholds.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good marks the other positive state that remains unavailable.

## cultivated fruitfulness
- reading: Productive ease is a cultivated capacity that can include opening, pruning, practice, and renewed growth.
- mechanism: Opening the ground, growth, purification, and repeated worship turn productive ease into cultivation. Its signature is fruitful repeatability, not the absence of exertion.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B006: Productive increase anchors the focus as fruitfulness rather than comfort.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Splitting and opening supply the disruptive first action of cultivation.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success and continuance supply cultivation's durable yield.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase make purification productive rather than merely subtractive.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the repeatable practice through which formed capacity is exercised.

## durable good choice
- reading: Ease is capacity directed toward the useful and lasting course, even when its benefit is delayed.
- mechanism: Preference for what is near is set against delayed useful good and persistence. The focus's spacious means therefore become teleological: one is enabled to choose and sustain the more durable good, which need not feel easiest now.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B003: Breadth and means anchor ease as capacity whose value depends on its chosen end.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference by choice supplies the decision that directs available capacity.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the attraction of the immediately accessible option.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies temporal distance from immediate ease.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward useful good supplies the quality by which the later course is preferred.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Persistence supplies the time horizon on which that course proves easier in the fuller sense.

## legible transmission
- reading: The route is made recoverable across time by marks that become trace, record, and succession.
- mechanism: The early visible sign develops into a distinguishing marker, a remaining trace, written pages, and succession. Ease becomes a transmissible itinerary whose discrete marks let later carriers recover and continue an earlier course.
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B008: Separated lines and marks anchor the focus as an articulated route.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible sign initiates the route's legibility.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides turns visibility into usable orientation.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign carries information from a prior passage into the present.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written page stabilizes marks into a retrievable record.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Repetition of the written-page cue makes recorded transmission recur across adjacent references.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant succession branch supplies one carrier following another without collapsing their distinct moments.

## allotted share
- reading: Facilitation assigns the addressee a fitting share within a larger distributed undertaking.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B007: Lots and apportioned shares anchor an unusual reading of facilitation as assignment of a fitting portion.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B010: A container gathering lots supplies ordered holding rather than uncontrolled scattering.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B012: Partners separating according to shares supplies the completed distribution.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting one thing first by selection changes random allotment into directed precedence.

## lateral course
- reading: The addressee is redirected onto a lateral course whose viability comes from changing direction.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B004: The left side and turning left anchor ease as a change of direction rather than a reduction of resistance.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B004: Orientation toward a direction supplies the act of taking a course.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B002: Direction, manner, and intended course supply purposeful rather than accidental turning.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B005: Leading something to the side supplies the lateral displacement mechanism.

## relay follower
- reading: The addressee is made the agile next leg in a chain that carries the reminder forward.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Quick, pliant following anchors the addressee as an agile next carrier.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B007: The runner following the leader supplies an ordered next position.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B006: Following the predecessor in a race repeats the relay structure under the split cognate mapping.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B004: Traveling in a predecessor's trace supplies continuity of course.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: One thing succeeding another extends the relay beyond a single pair.


# Focus 87:9

## reactivation
- reading: Cause a latent matter to become present again when that reactivation can move its recipient toward good rather than harm.
- mechanism: An external act of reminding makes something internally held present again; the repeated root links the initiated act to the reminder-event that may produce benefit.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall after lapse or alongside retention supplies the latent content that the imperative reactivates.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder as that which restores presence makes الذكرى the operative instrument rather than merely a topic.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm supplies the outcome by which successful reactivation is recognized.

## effect gate
- reading: Treat voiced reminder as an intervention whose use is answerable to whether it actually helps.
- mechanism: The verse does not merely command speech; it makes the reminder's efficacy a live condition. On a restrictive reading, benefit gates deployment or continuation of audible mention.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue supplies the public act whose practical effect is being tested.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as help toward good makes realized effect, not mere delivery, the conditional criterion.

## documentary reassertion
- reading: Re-present a still-valid claim so that what was dormant can regain practical force.
- mechanism: Reminding can be imagined as re-presenting a standing claim: benefit occurs when an obscured entitlement, obligation, or truth is made actionable again.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B008: The legal-instrument image supplies a durable claim that can be produced again when recognition has lapsed.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Practical benefit supplies the change from dormant claim to effective good.

## calibrated guidance
- reading: Offer a measured, creature-fitted act of recall as one local means by which guidance reaches its proper course.
- mechanism: The reminder becomes a local continuation of nurture, measured ordering, and gentle direction: its benefit lies in fitting guidance to a creaturely measure, not in maximizing undifferentiated repetition.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The focus reminder supplies the human-scale act that restores a matter to presence.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the criterion by which the fitted intervention is judged.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion supply a developmental frame for reminder rather than a merely verbal one.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Measured governance supplies calibration: an intervention is arranged with regard to limit and circumstance.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the directional function that reminding can serve.

## perishable window
- reading: Remind within a perishable window of receptivity, before potentially nourishing attention collapses into material that no longer takes benefit.
- mechanism: Useful growth emerges, feeds, and then becomes dark, weightless waste. Against that phase change, reminder is time-sensitive: benefit may depend on reaching attention while receptive material can still nourish.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder supplies the intervention whose timing is being reconceived.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the nutritive or helpful outcome that can be gained or missed.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies the onset of an available window.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing supply a concrete phase in which what emerged can nourish.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming brittle refuse supplies the loss of former usefulness.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Darkening supplies the visible terminal state of the transformation.

## preserved public relay
- reading: Relay what has been preserved into public speech; concealed receptivity can make it beneficial even when the human speaker cannot certify that outcome beforehand.
- mechanism: A recitation is given and protected from lapse, then divine knowledge spans what is public and concealed. The focus imperative is therefore a relay from preserved reception into public mention, while its hidden uptake need not be known in advance by the human reminder.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall and retention anchor the focus act in the preservation problem raised immediately before it.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue supplies the outward relay from held content to hearers.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Recitation and causing-to-recite supply the received content that can later be voiced as reminder.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance supplies the failure against which preservation and renewed recall operate.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Full disclosure to the knower supplies access to response conditions unavailable to the human speaker.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Open declaration and raised voice supply the observable side of reminder.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the inward response that can remain invisible while still becoming beneficial.

## enabled ease
- reading: Beneficial reminding is itself part of a course made open, light, and directionally workable.
- mechanism: The reminder is nested inside an enabled course of ease. Benefit can describe fit with a path made open and movement made compliant, not only a calculable payoff after speech.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder supplies a concrete operation within the enabled course.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the good toward which the eased operation is fitted.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supply the traversable course into which reminder enters.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light, compliant movement supplies an embodied image for an intervention that helps progress rather than obstructs it.

## receptive selection
- reading: The same reminder becomes beneficial where awe makes room for recall and is evaded where the recipient moves itself aside.
- mechanism: The following pair displays opposed receptor states: awe permits recollection, while the most wretched actively moves to the side. On the selective reading, إن نفعت points to receptivity rather than to intrinsic weakness in the reminder.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the positive effect whose variation is attributed to the receiving state.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Actual recollection supplies the realized beneficial response on one branch of reception.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear with awe supplies the receptive disposition that lets the reminder enter.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Deliberate distancing supplies the contrary receptor action, not a defect in the reminder's content.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposed to flourishing supplies the condition associated with avoidance.

## diagnostic division
- reading: Remind because encounter can disclose both recoverable memory and active self-removal; rejection does not by itself erase every kind of benefit.
- mechanism: A competing reading treats recollection and avoidance as two disclosures caused by encounter with the reminder. Its benefit need not mean universal acceptance; it can include making a hidden alignment behaviorally legible.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder supplies the common stimulus before the responses divide.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit is broadened from immediate uptake to the good of exposed response and differentiated consequence.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder restoring presence supplies the positive disclosed response.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Active removal to the side makes rejection visible as an event rather than leaving it latent.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Hardship and strenuous resistance supply the cost-bearing negative response disclosed by the encounter.

## livability rescue
- reading: A reminder benefits when it helps preserve the possibility of genuine life and interrupts movement toward a state devoid of both release and flourishing.
- mechanism: The aftermath depicts exposure to fire and a suspended state that is neither death nor life. Since a life-branch itself names benefit and good, نفع in the focus becomes preservation or recovery of livability, not mere informational gain.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm anchors the focus condition in a consequential good.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Encounter with fire and its heat supplies the harmful trajectory that beneficial reminder may interrupt.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the consuming environment at the end of failed reception.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of force and life supplies one denied terminus of the suspended condition.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good directly thickens نفع into viable flourishing rather than bare survival.

## embodied response chain
- reading: Benefit is a propagated change: recollection becomes named orientation, embodied practice, purification, and durable success.
- mechanism: Success and purification are followed by remembering a named Lord and prayer. The focus reminder therefore initiates a response chain from restored presence, through voiced naming, into embodied worship and transformed condition.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back to mind supplies the first internal movement in the response chain.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the chain's practical transformation rather than mere recollection.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Attainment and lasting success supply the completed positive outcome.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness supply the changed condition through which benefit becomes visible.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Verbal mention supplies the passage from inward recovery to outward articulation.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mark-image makes the name an identifying trace through which recalled orientation becomes explicit.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the embodied action into which verbal remembrance resolves.

## temporal reweighting
- reading: A reminder benefits by making a delayed and lasting good present enough to overcome the decision weight of what is merely near.
- mechanism: The later contrast diagnoses preference for what is near over what is later, better, and lasting. Reminder's benefit is thus a correction of temporal weighting: it restores effective presence to a good discounted because it is not proximal.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing an absent matter to mind supplies the operation that can counter proximity bias.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the true good against which short-horizon preference is reassessed.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference by choice supplies the weighting operation that reminder must alter.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the salience advantage enjoyed by present life.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the distance that causes the better object to be discounted.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the corrected evaluative direction.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and persistence supply the long horizon on which genuine benefit is measured.

## transmissible record
- reading: Reminding reactivates a transmissible record whose force can persist through writing and become newly vocal across generations.
- mechanism: What is voiced in the focus is later located in written sheets from an earlier horizon. Reminder becomes medium-transitive: a durable record can reactivate the same claim across absences, generations, and fresh acts of speech.
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue supplies the present oral realization of reminder.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B008: The documentary branch supplies the possibility that reminder carries standing force beyond a single utterance.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Persistence supplies the temporal durability required for repeated reactivation.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies the material carrier that survives the original speech event.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Collected sheets supply transmission through an organized body rather than an isolated inscription.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Priority and beginning supply the earlier horizon from which the record reaches the present.

## waterskin reinforcement
- reading: A reminder can function like a reinforcing strip: it helps an already filled capacity keep holding what would otherwise escape through lapse.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B002: The side strip on a waterskin supplies a reinforcing piece that preserves the vessel's capacity.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Retention and recall supply the human capacity figuratively treated as a vessel needing reinforcement.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B008: Hollows and level places that retain water supply the context's second containment image.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering supplies the content collected into the figurative vessel.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Lapse and loss of remembrance supply the leakage-like failure that reinforcement counters.

## guiding staff
- reading: The reminder may benefit as a staff-like aid that helps a person keep, test, or recover movement along a guided course.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B003: The staff supplies an instrument that supports movement, tests footing, or redirects a traveler.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder supplies the intervention figuratively carried as a practical aid.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the route on which the aid operates.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light and compliant motion supplies the changed gait made possible by support.

## document of right
- reading: Reminder re-presents a durable claim whose recognition can restore an obscured obligation, entitlement, or truth to action.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B008: A documentary instrument of right supplies a standing claim that can be produced when acknowledgment fails.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the restoration of practical force when the claim is recognized.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Persistence supplies the standing validity of the claim beyond its first presentation.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet supplies a recoverable material record.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome and final implication supplies the old record's renewed relevance in a later situation.

## sharp furrow
- reading: Some beneficial reminder may cut a furrow through settled attention, creating the opening in which purification and growth can begin.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B002: Hardness and sharp force supply the reminder's disruptive edge.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit constrains disruption to an opening ordered toward good rather than harm.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Splitting and cutting supply the opening action.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The plowman who cuts the earth turns the opening into a cultivation mechanism.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Increase and growth supply the productive result of the figurative furrow.


# Focus 87:10

## recall under awe
- reading: It identifies awe as the disposition through which latent retention becomes active recollection.
- mechanism: Reverent fear creates enough inward pressure for retained or neglected material to return to active awareness; the verse presents awe as a disposition that makes recollection occur.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back to mind supplies the active recovery event performed by the subject.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with reverent awe supplies the receptive pressure that conditions the recovery.

## epistemic apprehension
- reading: Apprehensive knowledge recognizes the stakes and thereby calls dormant knowledge back into presence.
- mechanism: The subject apprehends enough of a consequence or reality to retrieve what was already available but inactive; fear and knowledge remain co-present rather than mutually exclusive glosses.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall supplies the transition from latent retention to present cognition.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B002: Figurative knowing makes apprehension an epistemic trigger, not only an emotion.

## uttered response
- reading: The awed subject may carry recollection across the threshold into verbal acknowledgment.
- mechanism: What begins as inward recollection can cross into naming or avowal on the tongue; the focus may describe an awed response becoming expressible, not only a silent mental event.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue opens an outward verbal phase of the remembering response.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent awe supplies the pressure under which inward recognition becomes voiced.

## marked measure recognition
- reading: The subject apprehensively recognizes a guiding measure already marked into the ordered world.
- mechanism: A visible mark, measured formation, completed proportion, deliberate apportioning, and gentle guidance form a signal system. The apprehending subject does not merely retrieve words but recognizes guidance already impressed into an ordered creation.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back to mind makes recognition the focus event.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B002: The knowing branch makes the subject capable of apprehending an encoded order.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mapped image of a visible identifying mark supplies creation with a readable signature.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Estimating and measuring supply an intentional template behind what is made.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion make the template visibly coherent.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate ordering by measure supplies design rather than accidental arrangement.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a way turns measured order into guidance that can be recognized.

## residue reactivation
- reading: Remembering reconstructs a still-legible significance from what remains after visible vitality has collapsed.
- mechanism: Pasture emerges, is consumed by change, and becomes dark stubble; the hidden-extraction branch lets remembrance operate like recovering a meaningful pattern from residue after its vivid surface has vanished.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall supplies recovery of something no longer immediately present.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Awe supplies sensitivity to the loss and transformation that provoke recovery.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Drawing something out of concealment supplies the recovery motion.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the initially living and useful pattern that will be transformed.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry stubble supplies the residual state from which significance must be recovered.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark coloration marks the collapse of fresh visibility without requiring total disappearance.

## recited gathering
- reading: The person participates in a prior act of recitative gathering that moves content from hidden latency into renewed presence.
- mechanism: Recitation gathers what could disperse into forgetting and carries it across the hidden-visible boundary under encompassing knowledge. Focus remembrance becomes enabled re-collection rather than an isolated act of unaided memory.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall supplies the focus event whose enabling conditions are being clarified.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and assembling make recitation a re-collection of potentially scattered content.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reciting and causing recitation supply the voiced medium through which the gathering occurs.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss from memory supplies the dispersive condition that recitation and recall overcome.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower places manifest and latent content within a wider field of knowledge.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Visible manifestation supplies the exposed pole across which remembered content moves.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the latent pole from which content can be brought back.

## eased beneficial uptake
- reading: An eased and beneficial reminder becomes self-recollection in a recipient whose awe makes the offered content usable.
- mechanism: Ease opens a path, an external reminder presents content, benefit distinguishes successful reception, and the reflexive focus verb marks the recipient's own uptake. Khashya identifies responsiveness within an enabling relay rather than acting as a lone mechanical cause.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Active recollection supplies the recipient-side completion of the relay.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent awe supplies the recipient disposition that makes uptake possible.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supply an enabled route toward response.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: An offered reminder supplies the external input that the focus subject internalizes.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as the opposite of harm supplies the criterion by which uptake becomes effective.

## awe turns toward
- reading: Reverent fear is the capacity to stay turned toward a difficult reminder until it becomes one's own recollection.
- mechanism: The next subject moves to the side and away, whereas the focus subject lets the reminder enter and become recollection. The contrast reverses the expectation that fear necessarily causes recoil: reverent fear can orient toward an address, while wretchedness performs avoidance.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall marks reception and inward approach to the reminder.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Awe supplies a fear that remains receptive rather than fleeing.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Withdrawal and self-distancing supply the opposed bodily orientation.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness supplies the recipient condition associated with avoidance rather than uptake.

## transformable life
- reading: Remembering is a sign that the subject is still capable of a beneficial change of state rather than being fixed in sterile suspension.
- mechanism: The remembering subject remains capable of a beneficial change of state, in contrast to the following suspended condition under fire that resolves into neither death nor life. Remembering becomes evidence of moral-cognitive transformability rather than content retrieval alone.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall supplies a live transition from latency to active awareness.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Heating that can straighten or set a thing supplies a severe transformation image.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the environment in which the opposed subject is held.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Quiescence and extinguished activity supply one blocked endpoint.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good supplies the other endpoint that the suspended condition fails to reach.

## recollection to practice
- reading: Focus recollection is the hinge by which awe becomes voiced acknowledgment, worship, and purifying growth.
- mechanism: Purifying growth frames a sequence in which inward recollection becomes voiced naming and then embodied worship, with flourishing as its larger result. The focus verse is a threshold in a response process rather than its endpoint.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B004: The voiced-mention branch lets focus recollection open toward outward acknowledgment.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent awe supplies the inward disposition that initiates the response.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Winning and enduring supply the larger outcome of the response sequence.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase make purification productive rather than merely subtractive.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness supply the changed condition in which recollection can bear action.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Verbal mention supplies the explicit outward continuation of focus remembering.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: A name as indication gives voiced remembrance a determinate referent.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specific worship supplies the embodied action into which voiced remembrance proceeds.

## temporal counterselection
- reading: Remembering also makes a delayed and enduring future operative now, functioning as a counterselection against the merely near.
- mechanism: Choice favors what is near, while the later horizon is delayed, beneficial, and enduring. Apprehensive recollection carries that delayed horizon into present awareness, counterweighting immediate salience and revising preference.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall makes a non-present horizon cognitively available for present choice.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B002: Apprehensive knowing supplies awareness of consequences beyond immediate appearance.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preferential choice supplies the decision process that recollection can reweight.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the immediate horizon whose salience biases preference.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the horizon absent from immediate perception.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward what is beneficial supplies the corrected value direction.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Endurance supplies the temporal depth that outweighs merely near value.

## inscribed recurrence
- reading: The subject's recollection is a present recurrence of a pattern retained in records before this encounter.
- mechanism: Repeated written sheets and priority make the focus recollection a recurrence of something already inscribed and transmitted. The subject's insight is new as uptake but old as a durable communicative pattern.
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall supplies renewed presence of content that precedes the present recipient.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies durable external retention across recipients and times.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and temporal priority place the remembered pattern before the present address.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet image makes continuity concrete rather than merely asserted.

## dryness exposes residue
- reading: As a contained material overlay, khashya is a contraction that removes lush distraction and leaves the durable residue available for recollection.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B004: Shriveling and dryness supply a contracted state in which transient freshness has been removed.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recall supplies the recovery of significance that survives the contraction.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Living pasture supplies the fresh state before contraction.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture reduced to dry stubble supplies the contextual material analogue.

## tempered remembrance
- reading: As a qualified material analogy, recollection under awe tempers awareness into a straight and decisive capacity to respond.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B002: Hardness, sharpness, and force supply an image of recollection acquiring a decisive edge.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent pressure supplies the condition under which resolve is tested.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and completion supply the shaped result.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Fire-treatment that straightens a thing supplies the tempering process.

## stirred preference
- reading: A reminder received in awe can stir preference out of fixation on the near and reorient it toward what endures.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder supplies the intervention that can unsettle an established preference.
