  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Awe supplies the receptivity that lets the intervention move the subject.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B002: The non-dominant image of stirring something from its place supplies kinetic displacement of preference.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the attractor from which preference must be dislodged.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Endurance supplies the more stable orientation toward which recollection can move choice.

## documentary claim
- reading: As a contained documentary analogy, the subject recognizes an older recorded claim as presently binding and consequential.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B008: A document recording a right supplies an image of remembered teaching as a prior claim becoming operative.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B002: Apprehensive knowing supplies recognition that the recorded claim bears on the subject.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies the documentary medium in which a prior claim can persist.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Collected sheets supply continuity and aggregation across multiple records.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an outcome lets the old record become present in its eventual consequence.


# Focus 87:11

## enacted estrangement
- reading: The maximally wretched person makes wretchedness visible by continually relocating himself to the far side of the feminine referent.
- mechanism: The side-root becomes a relation deliberately broken: the agent moves himself out of adjacency with the referent. Read with the wretchedness branch of the split ش ق و inventory, the superlative is not only a prior label but a condition enacted and exposed by this withdrawal.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Keeping aside, avoidance, and estrangement supply the verb's literal motion and make self-exile the model's operative relation.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposite happiness supplies the agent's superlative condition and lets the distancing action diagnose that condition.

## avoidance as toil
- reading: The one deepest in hardship labors to hold the referent at a distance; evasion is itself a toil-producing practice.
- mechanism: Avoidance is not effortless nonattendance. The split root makes it an exertion: the agent keeps working to preserve separation, so the attempted escape becomes part of the hardship named by the superlative.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Withdrawal and active removal to a side supply the distance that must be maintained.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Strenuous suffering and endurance supply the energetic cost of keeping the referent away.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Hardship, difficulty, and toil preserve the labor reading in the second mapped inventory rather than reducing ش ق و to a moral label.

## contested proximity
- reading: He sustains a side-boundary against something still close enough to press upon him; avoidance discloses contested nearness, not freedom from relation.
- mechanism: The referent is not simply absent. Avoidance reverses a live side-by-side relation, implying something close enough to require recurrent lateral repositioning. The verse can therefore stage contested proximity: the agent remains defined by what he tries to put aside.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B002: Nearness and companionship at one's side supply the latent proximity against which avoidance operates.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Removal and estrangement reverse that adjacency and turn a stable side into an actively defended boundary.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness names the person whose identity remains relationally bound to the object he excludes.

## counter guided course
- reading: The agent takes himself off a measured, indicated, and eased course; wretchedness appears as counter-guidance performed from within an already afforded path.
- mechanism: The context builds a directed world: shaping establishes orientation, measured governance sets a course, guidance gently indicates it, and easing opens movement along it. Against that sequence, the focus verb is not arbitrary avoidance but self-directed lateral motion across or away from an afforded route.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Removal to the side supplies the focus action that becomes counter-motion within the contextual course.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B004: Turning and intending a direction supply an oriented trajectory rather than undifferentiated movement.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Ordering by measure and deliberation supplies the course's fitted arrangement.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies positive direction and makes the focus side-step a counter-guidance.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supply an afforded passage that the focus agent nevertheless leaves.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light, compliant movement sharpens the contrast between being eased along and forcing oneself sideways.

## deliberate anti memory
- reading: A gathered and beneficial reminder reaches the field of memory, but the most wretched actively removes himself from it; the failure is resistant relation, not missing information.
- mechanism: Recitation gathers and presents material, non-forgetting blocks accidental loss, and the repeated remembrance sequence culminates in a fear-responsive recipient who recollects. The focus feminine pronoun then most immediately takes the reminder as its object, so avoidance becomes an active anti-memory relation rather than mere ignorance or lapse.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Deliberate withdrawal supplies the action that contrasts with recollection and excludes a merely cognitive lapse.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and bringing together supply a presented, coherent object from which the focus agent separates himself.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance supplies the cognitive failure explicitly blocked before the focus, forcing a more volitional account of avoidance.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder as that which makes one remember supplies the likely feminine antecedent and the object that remains available for uptake.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm supplies the reminder's offered efficacy, which avoidance declines rather than fails to encounter.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies the receptive disposition whose remembrance is contrasted with the focus agent's distancing.

## known evasion
- reading: The avoider may leave the reminder's side, but cannot move the act or its concealed motive outside knowledge; avoidance becomes exposed evasion.
- mechanism: The focus agent can move to the side of the reminder, but the preceding knowledge frame spans both manifest and hidden domains. Spatial or social evasion therefore fails as concealment: the motion away is itself disclosed even if its motive remains inward.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Distance, withdrawal, and foreignness supply the attempted evasion whose epistemic success is reversed.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure of a thing to the knower supplies the frame in which the avoider's displacement remains legible.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Manifest appearance to the eye supplies the public pole across which the act can be seen.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment and hiddenness supply the inward pole that does not escape the stated knowledge.

## failed flank shield
- reading: The self-protective side maneuver fails: the next state reaches the very flank imagined as guarded, turning avoidance into exposure.
- mechanism: A remote focus branch imagines a shield held at the side, while the immediately following fire-root inventory joins direct fire contact to the back or flank. The attempted lateral defense is reversed: putting the reminder off to one's side does not shield the agent but leads into exposure of the very bodily side being guarded.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B011: A shield or cover held at the side supplies avoidance as a defensive posture rather than neutral distance.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B007: Flank pain or injury supplies the bodily vulnerability that the imagined side-shield attempts to protect.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: Meeting fire and its heat supplies the consequence that defeats the defensive reading of avoidance.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B005: The back-and-flank body part supplies a precise bodily hinge between the focus side image and the following fire contact.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Blazing fire and branding supply invasive contact rather than a threat kept safely outside the boundary.

## suspended toil
- reading: The superlative names a toil that cannot resolve into either extinction or flourishing; avoidance opens an enduring suspended condition.
- mechanism: The hardship branch of ٱلْأَشْقَى is temporally expanded by the paired negations that follow. Neither loss of life nor beneficial life completes the process, so the avoider's toil becomes unresolved persistence rather than a severe but terminating episode.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Strenuous hardship and endurance supply the focus condition that the next verse stretches through time.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Departure of force and life supplies one unavailable endpoint of the hardship.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Stillness and extinguishing supply a second image of cessation that the negation withholds.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good supplies the opposite endpoint also denied, distinguishing bare continuation from flourishing.

## counter practice
- reading: The verse catches a self-forming practice in motion: the wretched agent repeatedly trains himself away from reminder, opposite the cultivation-remembrance-prayer sequence.
- mechanism: The successful counterpart is built through actions: self-purification grows and cleans, remembrance restores presence, and prayer establishes worship. This revises the focus superlative from a fixed type into the outcome of an opposite self-practice: one agent works on himself toward fitness, while the other works himself sideways from the reminder.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Self-removal and estrangement supply the negative practice that can be compared with self-purification.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness supplies the resulting agent-description, now read dynamically rather than as unexplained essence.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success and lasting attainment supply the positive outcome opposed to wretchedness.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply self-directed cultivation rather than self-directed displacement.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness supply the fitting condition produced by the positive practice.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something present after forgetting supplies receptive recollection as the opposite of active avoidance.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the embodied culmination of the alternative sequence.

## preference topology
- reading: Avoidance is a preference topology: the near life is kept as side-companion while the beneficial and lasting horizon is pushed away.
- mechanism: The later contrast redescribes avoidance as an allocation of proximity. Preference draws the near life to one's side, while the delayed, beneficial, enduring term is placed away. The focus agent does not merely flee; he arranges companions and horizons, attaching himself to what is near and lateralizing what lasts.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B002: Nearness and companionship at one's side supply the positive placement granted to the preferred term.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Removal and estrangement supply the complementary placement imposed on the reminder and its horizon.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Choosing one thing in preference supplies the selection rule that turns avoidance into relational allocation.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B006: Taking something exclusively for oneself sharpens the self-enclosing side of the preference.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Closeness and approach supply the immediate term pulled into companionship.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the horizon that can be kept psychologically offside.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the value that the enacted preference misorders.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and remaining supply the long horizon excluded by attachment to the near.

## durable recurrence
- reading: He can only change his own position relative to an inscribed, recurrent reminder that precedes him and continues toward an outcome.
- mechanism: The closing attestation gives the avoided reminder a durable and recurrent medium. Written sheets preserve it from the agent's withdrawal, while beginning, return-to-outcome, and succession make the message precede this encounter and continue beyond it. Avoidance changes position, not the reminder's availability or historical persistence.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Withdrawal and foreignness supply the local attempt to sever relation with the reminder.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies durable inscription that remains when a hearer moves away.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply a history earlier than the focus agent's act.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome or final consequence turns firstness into a temporal arc that can outlast immediate evasion.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: One thing following another supplies transmission and recurrence from a non-dominant mapped branch.

## ecological margin
- reading: As a contained ecological echo, the avoider takes a marginal side-position within a world where nourishing growth is sorted into rooted remainder or dark carried-off debris.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B010: Low perennial shrub growth supplies a marginal, persisting plant-position within the focus root.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing growth supply the productive field from which material can later be sorted.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Scum floating and carried by floodwater supplies expelled, mobile residue rather than rooted persistence.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture become dry debris supplies the transformation that makes ecological side-taking consequential.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or dark redness supplies the terminal material condition of the transformed growth.

## avoidance as mark
- reading: The act of withdrawing from the reminder becomes the visible trace by which extreme wretchedness identifies itself.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Active withdrawal supplies the observable behavior that can leave or constitute a diagnostic mark.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness supplies the condition identified by that behavior rather than an arbitrary external label.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible impressed mark by which a thing is known supplies the non-dominant marking mechanism.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing trace that guides recognition supplies the epistemic function of the avoider's act.

## sidelined runner
- reading: In a qualified race-course echo, he does not disappear but assigns himself the losing side-position, parallel to a course whose successful figure follows rather than sidesteps.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B005: A spare animal or captive led alongside supplies the focus agent's displaced, parallel position.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B003: Being outdone in a hardship contest supplies competitive loss within the side-position.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light, compliant movement supplies the available course that could have been followed.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B006: The runner immediately following the leader supplies the ordered position opposed to remaining a spare at the side.


# Focus 87:12

## direct exposure
- reading: The relative subject is placed in direct, sustained contact with a concretely burning and exceptionally great fire.
- mechanism: A person is brought into direct, suffering contact with a burning fire whose magnitude exceeds an ordinary instance.
- trace:
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: The exposure-to-heat branch supplies the subject's direct and painful contact with the fire.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: The burning-fire branch supplies the concrete consuming medium rather than light alone.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B001: The magnitude branch raises the fire above a merely small or ordinary punishment.

## transforming furnace
- reading: The fire is also a working furnace that exerts severe transformative force on the subject.
- mechanism: The great fire can function as an active furnace: it does not only surround the subject but works upon and forcibly alters what enters it.
- trace:
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: The kindling, roasting, and fire-straightening branch makes the encounter an operation performed on the subject.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Burning fire provides the material agent that can heat and transform.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B010: The hardship-and-heaviness branch makes the furnace's pressure grievous rather than beneficial by default.

## magnified enmity
- reading: It may also externalize magnified hostility and pride as the conflagration their bearer finally encounters.
- mechanism: Self-exaltation feeds interpersonal hostility until it becomes the large conflagration that its bearer must meet.
- trace:
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B007: The flaring-enmity branch allows the fire to carry a social conflagration alongside physical burning.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B006: The self-magnification branch supplies the conduct that enlarges the hostile fire.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: The non-dominant mapped branch keeps the resulting social image anchored in an actual encounter with fire and hardship.

## beacon trap
- reading: The destination can be read as a conspicuous lure whose approach culminates in capture.
- mechanism: A visible fire draws approach while concealing capture; its greatness is the dominance of a trap that closes around the approaching subject.
- trace:
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B005: The snare branch supplies capture concealed within the subject's movement toward the fire.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B003: The sighting-or-seeking-fire branch supplies a distant attractor that can solicit approach.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B002: The chief-share branch makes this trap the affair's dominant portion rather than an incidental hazard.

## bounded superlative
- reading: Its greatness is immense but bounded beneath the sovereignty and elevation established at the opening.
- mechanism: The opening establishes sovereign and vertical supremacy before the focus calls the fire great. That ordering bounds the fire's superlative: it is greatest within its punitive field, not a rival absolute.
- trace:
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Sovereignty and lordship establish the governing rank above every later agent or condition.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B001: Vertical elevation supplies the opening maximum against which the later greatness is relationally bounded.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B005: The rank-and-leadership branch lets greatness denote precedence within an ordered domain.

## measured route
- reading: It can appear as a marked destination reached within the same measured order, with the subject following a route rather than falling into chaos.
- mechanism: Creation, proportion, measured governance, and gentle direction make the focus destination legible as part of an ordered route. The fire is not a random outbreak; even arrival at it can occur through a measured sequence of following signs.
- trace:
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measurement and proportion supply a designed frame rather than an accidental environment.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Integrity and straight formation make the route part of a coherent order.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate apportioning supplies governance of stages and outcomes.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies directional movement.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B007: The race-follower branch anchors the subject as one proceeding closely behind a prior lead.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B005: The beacon-and-landmark branch makes the fire a marked terminus on that route.

## pasture to residue
- reading: The fire also echoes a wider conversion cycle, actively reducing vitality and formed condition toward dark residue.
- mechanism: Pasture is brought forth, converted into worthless dried matter, and darkened. This material sequence activates the furnace baseline: the focus fire is a terminal conversion process that reduces formed vitality to residue.
- trace:
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Grazing vegetation supplies the living material at the start of the conversion.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: State-transformation supplies the causal hinge from vitality to another condition.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry, debris-like matter supplies the degraded output.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Darkening supplies the visible material signature of the completed conversion.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Roasting and fire-working anchor the ecological sequence back in the focus as thermal transformation.

## forced disclosure
- reading: The great fire can also force epistemic exposure, making a concealed or confused condition glaringly manifest.
- mechanism: Knowledge spans what is manifest and hidden, while remembrance recovers what can lapse. Against that field, the fire-root's illumination and confusion branches make the great fire a forced disclosure in which obscurity fails and the subject's condition becomes impossible to hide.
- trace:
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower supplies the cognitive frame in which hidden condition is already available.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Visible manifestation supplies the exposed side of the contrast.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B003: Removal of concealment turns hiddenness into an event of disclosure.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recovered remembrance supplies the responsive alternative to cognitive concealment.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B001: Illumination lets the fire expose rather than only burn.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B010: The misleading-confusion branch supplies the obscurity that the greater disclosure can reverse.

## avoidance inside fire
- reading: Avoidance becomes self-reversing motion: fleeing the reminder culminates in a fire whose own condition is futile flight and instability.
- mechanism: The most wretched moves aside from the reminder, but aversion and flight also reside inside the fire root's inventory. Avoidance therefore rebounds: movement meant to create distance becomes the motion by which the subject enters a condition of futile recoil.
- trace:
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Active distancing and withdrawal supply the subject's initial lateral motion away from the reminder.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Severe hardship supplies the condition characterizing the avoidant subject.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B006: Aversion, flight, and instability relocate avoidance inside the semantic field of the fire itself.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Actual exposure to fire closes the attempted escape in direct encounter.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B010: Greatness as unbearable hardship makes recoil persistent rather than successful.

## arrested consumption
- reading: Its terror lies partly in noncompletion: it keeps the subject between extinguished power and unavailable life.
- mechanism: The post-focus denial of both death and life prevents the burning encounter from reaching either terminal consumption or restored vitality. The fire becomes a process held open, repeatedly removing power without granting extinction.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of force and life supplies the endpoint that the following negation withholds.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Quiescence and extinguishing supply a second form of cessation that remains unavailable.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B006: Preservation of life supplies the minimal continuation that can coexist with denied flourishing.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: Enduring fire and hardship anchor the suspended state in ongoing exposure.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B010: Heaviness and hardship make duration, not only temperature, a dimension of the fire's greatness.

## counter ritual
- reading: The fire encounter can sound like the compulsory negative counterpart of refused remembrance, purification, and embodied prayer.
- mechanism: Success, self-purification, remembrance, and chosen prayer form one sequence; the focus's cognate split inventory joins prayer to fire-exposure and fire-straightening. The two sequences become asymmetric rites: voluntary responsive worship versus compulsory subjection to a severe furnace.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Attained success and continuance supply the outcome of the voluntary sequence.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purification supplies an inward alteration chosen before punishment.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Active recollection supplies the cognitive initiation of worship.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the voluntary bodily response paired with remembrance.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: Binding worship in the focus root preserves a ritual resonance inside the fire-facing verb.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Fire-straightening supplies a coercive bodily counterpart to voluntary purification.

## near choice lure
- reading: The preference itself can operate like bait: nearness draws the chooser out, and the apparently advantageous route closes as the great-fire trap.
- mechanism: Preference selects the near life, while a rare good-root branch depicts drawing an animal from its shelter. Joined to the focus snare and distant-fire branches, immediate preference becomes bait: what looks near and advantageous draws the chooser into a lasting trap.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preferential choosing supplies the decision that commits the subject to the apparent advantage.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the bait's immediate accessibility.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B006: The rare luring-from-a-burrow branch supplies an extraction mechanism concealed inside apparent good.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration supplies the long horizon against which the near choice proves deceptive.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B005: The snare branch turns mistaken preference into capture.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B003: Fire sighted or sought from afar supplies the visible lure toward which the chooser moves.

## temporal greatness
- reading: Greatness also describes temporal dominance: the fire is a heavy, enduring share that overwhelms the short scale of near-world preference.
- mechanism: Laterness and endurance supply a temporal scale for الكُبْرَى. The fire is great not only by intensity or extent but by the heavy share it occupies beyond the short horizon of present choice.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the extended horizon absent from immediate preference.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Persistence and duration make temporal continuation an axis of comparison.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B002: The chief-share branch lets greatness denote the dominant portion of an affair over time.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B010: Heaviness makes that extended share experientially burdensome.

## precedent track
- reading: The subject may be the latest follower on an old, recorded track whose recurrent outcome is the great fire.
- mechanism: Written earlier records, return to an outcome, and succession activate the focus branch of the racer following immediately behind. The one who meets the fire can be read as following an already marked human track toward a recurring consequence.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet branch supplies durable recording of an earlier pattern.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an eventual outcome supplies recurrence rather than mere antiquity.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant mapped branch of one thing succeeding another supplies serial following across generations.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B007: The follower just behind a leader anchors the condemned subject in a pattern of close succession.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B005: The landmark branch makes the destination a historically marked point on the track.

## fire as counter script
- reading: The great fire becomes a counter-script that brands its own durable record onto the one exposed to it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B008: Smoke pigment and tattooing supply a fire-derived substance that leaves a bodily inscription.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining mark supplies persistence after the causal event.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Writing on a sheet supplies the contrasting model of an external readable record.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B002: The chief-share branch enlarges the mark into the dominant record borne by the subject.

## subject as fodder
- reading: As a contained analogy, the subject is repositioned from chooser or consumer to consumable fodder within the great fire's economy.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B010: The camel-grazed plant supplies an unexpected edible-material image inside the focus verb's root.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing place the plant image inside an explicit consumption ecology.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture reduced to dry waste supplies the transition from nourishment to expendable matter.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Burning fire converts the trophic image into a fuel-consumption analogy.

## compulsory anti prayer
- reading: With strict containment, the subject is made to enact a compulsory anti-prayer whose locus is the fire refused worship has not averted.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: Binding worship supplies ritual posture and obligation as a secondary resonance.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Encountering fire preserves the immediate syntactic sense and contains the ritual activation.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: The later prescribed worship supplies the successful voluntary counterpart.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purification distinguishes chosen alignment from punitive fire-working.


# Focus 87:13

## suspended endpoints
- reading: The line specifies an imposed interval in which death never finishes and life never becomes functioning animation.
- mechanism: The first negation prevents the loss of vitality from reaching death, while the second prevents persistence from becoming animate life. The subject is therefore held between terminal cessation and functioning animation rather than alternating normally between them.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of vitality supplies the terminal endpoint that the first negation refuses to let complete.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B003: Animate existence supplies the positive endpoint that the second negation withholds.

## persistence without welfare
- reading: Mere continuation is severed from life: the subject cannot shut down, yet receives none of life's benefit or rescue.
- mechanism: Negated death removes quiescent shutdown, while negated life removes benefit and saving good. What remains is duration without rest, welfare, rescue, or flourishing.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Death-like stillness, sleep, or wearing out supplies a possible shutdown that la yamutu denies.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit, good, and rescue supplies the qualitative life that wa-la yahya denies.

## no collapse no renewal
- reading: The verse can also deny both phases of a restorative rhythm: no merciful lapse and no return to freshness.
- mechanism: A death-word branch names a swoon or seizure from which one later recovers, while a life-word branch names earth renewed through rain and growth. Negating both yields an anti-cycle: no temporary collapse that grants interruption and no regenerative return.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B009: A recoverable faint or seizure supplies an episodic collapse whose interruption is denied.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B002: Rain-driven renewal and fresh growth supply the restorative phase that is also denied.

## measured anti completion
- reading: It becomes a measured anti-completion: an assigned outcome whose punishment is precisely the failure to attain either kind of completion.
- mechanism: Creation is followed by setting right, measure by guidance: each earlier pair moves a thing toward form, limit, and direction. Against that ordered movement, the focus state becomes an anti-completion deliberately situated within the same order, a creature brought to a condition that reaches neither terminal loss nor positive telos.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: The loss-of-vitality endpoint remains visibly blocked in the focus.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B003: Functioning animation remains the equally blocked positive endpoint.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B003: Completed and proportioned formation supplies the context's first image of arrival at integrity.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Intrinsic straightness and completion turns creation into directed completion rather than bare origination.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A measured limit supplies the notion that things ordinarily attain a determined bound.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a path supplies telic movement, sharpening the focus as its punitive inversion.

## arrested ecology
- reading: It also pictures matter denied both ecological outcomes: it cannot finish into inert waste and cannot re-enter fertile renewal.
- mechanism: The pasture sequence supplies a material cycle: emergence, usable growth, deliberate transformation, then withered debris. The focus roots contain matching images of revived earth and dead land, but both are negated. The punished subject is unlike vegetation that can complete decay and unlike earth that can receive renewal: the cycle arrests while exposure continues.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B003: Dead, unusable land supplies the completed lifeless condition that the first negation blocks.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B002: Earth revived into fresh growth supplies the regenerative condition that the second negation blocks.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: Emergence to the outside initiates the visible material cycle.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Grazable pasture supplies a living and useful phase rather than life in the abstract.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Causing a thing to become a new state makes the decay transition explicit.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture reduced to brittle waste supplies the normally completed death-side of the cycle.

## conscious nonresponse
- reading: He remains conscious enough that oblivion cannot release him, yet never becomes alive in the sense of receiving benefit, rescue, or transformative remembrance.
- mechanism: The context distinguishes loss of remembrance, received reminder, beneficial response, fear-filled recollection, and deliberate withdrawal. This revises nondeath as awareness that is not extinguished and nonlife as awareness that never becomes beneficial response. The subject remains available to apprehension but closed to transformation.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B006: Deadness of heart and understanding supplies the cognitive extinction that la yamutu can be heard to withhold.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and rescue supplies the fruitful responsiveness that wa-la yahya withholds.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of an item from remembrance supplies the cognitive disappearance explicitly negated earlier in the context.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder designed to reactivate memory supplies the offered stimulus.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as the opposite of harm distinguishes fruitful reception from mere exposure.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies the affect that lets one recipient actually remember.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Active distancing and withdrawal supplies the wretched recipient's contrary response.

## fire as nonterminal processing
- reading: Fire is the mechanism of an unfinished process: it continually acts and deadens, while the two negations prevent either destruction or restored life from closing the process.
- mechanism: The immediately preceding fire is not only a location but an acting medium: one meets its heat, and a branch of the same root depicts fire kindled to work or shape a thing. The double negation makes that action nonterminal. Fire can continually diminish strength and act on the subject, but it neither completes destruction nor produces restored animation.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B002: The deadening of force supplies what the fire can repeatedly do without achieving final death.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B003: Animate functioning supplies the positive condition that repeated processing never restores.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Direct encounter with fire and its heat supplies the immediate causal medium.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Kindling heat to work or set a thing supplies a process image rather than a merely static location.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Burning fire and its mark make ongoing material exposure concrete.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B001: Magnitude intensifies the acting medium while making its failure to terminate the subject more striking.

## blocked orientation
- reading: He cannot move toward life: he neither yields nor approaches the growth-remembrance-worship sequence that follows.
- mechanism: The verses after the focus define a positive trajectory as surviving success, growth or purification, recollection, and worship. Distant but live focus branches then yield an orientation reading: the subject neither yields to truth under the death-root nor comes forward under the life-root. His stasis is relational, the inability to enter the successful trajectory set immediately beside him.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B013: Submission to truth supplies a branch-distant yielding that the negated focus verb refuses.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B009: The summons to come or approach supplies positive movement that the second negation blocks.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success joined with survival supplies the attained condition contrasted with suspended nonlife.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply a positive developmental trajectory.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Active recollection supplies the inward turn that precedes worship.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specific worship supplies the outward act completing the positive trajectory.

## duration split from life
- reading: The paradox becomes diagnostic: he remains, but remaining has been emptied of benefit, rescue, and every quality for which life is chosen.
- mechanism: The later comparison separates chosen worldly life, superior good, and greater permanence into distinct values. That separation resolves the focus paradox without dissolving it: la yamutu grants ongoing duration, while wa-la yahya denies life as benefit and good. Persistence is present, but the evaluative content that would make it life is absent.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Blocked loss of vitality supplies continued duration at the focus.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit, good, and rescue supplies the quality denied despite that duration.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preferential choice makes life an evaluated object rather than a synonym for bare persistence.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: The named worldly life activates the benefit-bearing sense of the same focus root.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B002: The lower or worldly qualifier bounds the preferred life as one mode rather than life as mere existence.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness supplies the contrasting temporal domain in which quality and duration are compared.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies an explicit evaluative axis separate from duration.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance and permanence supply the temporal axis, proving that remaining and living need not be identical.

## recorded invariant
- reading: It is additionally framed as a fixed outcome, durable enough to recur as an inherited and written warning.
- mechanism: The closing appeal to repeated written sheets and an earlier source can extend the focus from an instantaneous sensation to a stable, transmissible outcome. The first-text root also carries return to an eventual outcome, allowing the suspended state to be read as an invariant terminus repeatedly attested rather than a passing anomaly.
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: The blocked terminal endpoint supplies one half of the stable proposition.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B003: The blocked animate endpoint supplies the proposition's other half.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies durable inscription rather than transient speech.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet cue reinforces transmission across named prior witnesses.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an eventual outcome activates the focus as a fixed terminus carried by earlier teaching.

## formal sparing without mercy
- reading: The subject is formally spared the killing endpoint, but the paired denial of life turns sparing into its punitive opposite: preservation without mercy.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Literal loss of life supplies the killing endpoint from which the subject is withheld.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B006: Sparing someone alive supplies the expected merciful consequence that the second negation cancels.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B003: Preservation as pardon or merciful sparing exposes how punitive mere preservation can become when detached from life.

## fire snare unresolved prey
- reading: The fire behaves like a snare holding wounded prey in an endlessly unresolved state: never certifiably dead, never restored to animate life.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B014: Checking whether wounded game is dead supplies an image of terminal status remaining perpetually unresolved.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B003: Animate functioning supplies the recovery that never resolves the uncertainty in the other direction.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B005: Snares and traps supply the enclosing mechanism through which the fire can be imaged as holding its quarry.


# Focus 87:14

## durable rectitude
- reading: The ayah announces lasting good as already realized in the kind of person who actively brings the self into purity and rectitude.
- mechanism: Success with lasting good and self-purification form an outcome-process relation: the successful subject is identified by actively bringing the self into purity and rectitude.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: The durable-success branch supplies an achieved outcome that exceeds a momentary win.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: The purity-and-rectitude branch supplies the reflexive transformation that characterizes the subject.

## cultivated increase
- reading: Purification is self-cultivation: worked openness produces living increase, and falah names the resulting yield.
- mechanism: A tiller opens worked ground so that increase can occur; read together, the branches make self-purification a cultivation process and success its viable yield.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The ground-splitting tiller supplies productive labor and prepared soil as the success mechanism.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: The increase branch supplies the growth that answers cultivation rather than a merely subtractive cleansing.

## opening into fitness
- reading: Success is an emergence: self-fitting and the opening of a viable passage are two sides of the same event.
- mechanism: Fitness and opening make a threshold model: as the subject makes the self apt for passage, a constricted route opens into successful emergence.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: The cleaving branch supplies an opened way through material that had enclosed or narrowed it.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B004: The fittingness branch makes the reflexive act an acquisition of aptitude for that passage.

## worship enactment
- reading: The subject's rectification becomes audible and bodily as naming, remembrance, and prayer within the Lord's formative care.
- mechanism: The opening summons to worship and the post-focus remembering and prayer frame tazakka as an inward change that becomes enacted recognition, not private moral hygiene.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Durable success remains the focus outcome around which the worship frame closes.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude anchor the inward side of the enacted sequence.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: The worship branch supplies the initial imperative whose performance is resumed after the focus.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: The naming branch supplies explicit designation as the object of both the opening summons and later remembrance.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture, repair, and completion place self-rectification within an ongoing formative relation.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Remembrance passing onto the tongue supplies the outward articulation immediately after tazakka.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: The specific worship branch supplies bodily practice as the completion of the post-focus sequence.

## enabled maturation
- reading: The subject participates reflexively in a measured, guided maturation whose conditions and direction precede the subject's own work.
- mechanism: Creation, proportioning, measured limits, guidance, and nurturing growth turn the reflexive tazakka from autonomous self-production into maturation enabled by prior formation and direction.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The tiller supplies cultivation as a cooperative use of conditions not created by the crop itself.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth supplies the maturation whose agency the preceding sequence reassigns.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion supply a formative agency surrounding the subject's reflexive work.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant mapped branch contributes feeding and emergence, sharpening the maturation model without replacing the source root.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measured formation supplies an antecedent design within which self-growth can become apt.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion supply a trajectory toward wholeness rather than mere removal.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A measure reaching its proper limit supplies bounded development rather than indefinite increase.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction supplies the route by which measured potential becomes realized growth.

## growth sorted from residue
- reading: Successful growth is cultivated and purified growth: increase is tested by what remains viable rather than by how lushly it first appears.
- mechanism: The pasture is brought from concealment into growth and then made dark debris; against that cycle, tazakka cannot mean undifferentiated increase, but a purifying cultivation that distinguishes viable yield from growth destined to become residue.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The tiller anchors the focus in the same agricultural domain as pasture and its fate.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Increase supplies the initially positive growth that the decay sequence forces the reader to qualify.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purification supplies selection or rectification within growth, preventing increase alone from defining success.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Emergence from concealment supplies the first phase of visible flourishing.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the living crop and nourishment on which the cultivation model operates.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Transformation into another condition supplies the directional change from growth to spent matter.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture reduced to dry debris supplies the failed endpoint of growth that does not endure.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark coloration makes the terminal state sensorially distinct from living increase.

## opened guided route
- reading: Purification makes the subject fit for a guided passage through real limits, while falah is the opening and successful traversal of that passage.
- mechanism: Measured constriction, guidance, and an opening into ease activate the focus as a route: becoming fit does not erase limits but permits movement through them into a viable way.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving supplies the passage opened through a narrow or resistant boundary.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B004: Fittingness supplies the subject's acquired aptitude to traverse the opened route.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B004: Narrowing something to a limited measure supplies the constricted geometry of the route.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication supplies direction through the measured limit.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supplies the destination and confirms that constriction is traversed, not denied.

## memory feedback
- reading: Tazakka is maintained through a loop of receiving, retaining, recalling, benefiting, and responding until rectitude becomes durable.
- mechanism: Recitation is retained against forgetting, made tractable, issued again as reminder, received as benefit, and recollected by the responsive subject; tazakka becomes a recursive retention-response process rather than a single cleansing event.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Lasting good supplies the persistence that the memory sequence explains operationally.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Rectification anchors the repeated cognitive and responsive operations in the focus.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Recitation and causing-to-recite supply the incoming content of the feedback process.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembered content, negated in the source, supplies the failure mode against which retention is promised.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light, tractable movement supplies the transition from retained content to ready practice.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder as that which reactivates memory supplies the repeated input to other subjects.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit supplies the criterion that distinguishes live reception from mere exposure.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Active recollection supplies the receiver's internal return of what the reminder presented.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent fear supplies the receptive disposition that lets recollection alter conduct.

## hidden manifest integrity
- reading: Purification is hidden-manifest integrity: voiced and embodied worship matters as the expression of an inward state, not as its replacement.
- mechanism: Knowledge spans what is manifest and concealed, while remembrance and prayer give public form to the post-focus subject; tazakka therefore requires integrity between an inward state already exposed to knowledge and its outward enactment.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success remains the focus judgment, now tested across hidden and manifest dimensions.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity anchors the internal condition whose congruence with outward action is at issue.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to the knower supplies epistemic access that does not stop at performance.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Visible manifestation supplies the exterior pole of the integrity test.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the interior pole that outward observance cannot substitute for.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Spoken remembrance gives the purified state an audible manifestation.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Worship gives that state a visible embodied manifestation.

## avoidance antimodel
- reading: Purification is not generalized avoidance: it is turning toward reminder and regaining the capacity to receive, recollect, and enact life-giving good.
- mechanism: The responsive subject recollects, while the wretched subject moves aside from reminder and reaches a state deprived of both death's release and life's benefit; the focus then names tazakka as the counter-trajectory of restored receptivity and living response.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success with lasting good supplies the positive endpoint opposed to stalled nonlife.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Rectification supplies the inward turn that reverses evasive movement.
  - 87:10 **سَيَذَّكَّرُ** ذ ك ر B003: Recollection supplies the positive response available before the negative trajectory.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Reverent fear supplies responsiveness rather than flight.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Self-distancing and withdrawal supply the opposed movement away from reminder.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness as the contrary of flourishing labels the subject produced by persistent avoidance.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: Contact with consuming heat supplies the destination of the avoidance trajectory.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of force and life, explicitly negated as completion, supplies one unavailable endpoint.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good, also negated, supplies the positive capacity missing from the stalled state.

## choice of duration
- reading: Purification recalibrates preference itself, allowing a real but near benefit to be judged against a better and more enduring horizon.
- mechanism: Preference selects the proximate life, while the later domain is named more beneficial and more enduring; this makes tazakka a reordering of valuation across time and makes falah the durable result of choosing beyond immediacy.
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Lasting good supplies the focus criterion that the following comparison explicitly confirms.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Rectification supplies the internal reordering needed to resist a merely proximate preference.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Deliberate preference supplies the choice operation whose criterion must be purified.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life construed as benefit supplies the attractive good being evaluated rather than treating the near life as valueless.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Proximity supplies the immediacy bias that makes the nearer benefit disproportionately salient.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the competing horizon that preference tends to discount.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies qualitative superiority rather than duration alone.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance supplies temporal superiority and resolves the lasting component of falah.

## oral aperture
- reading: The purified subject is imaged as an opened mouth through which concealed orientation becomes voiced remembrance.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B002: The lip-cleft branch supplies a bodily aperture directly anchored in the success verb.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: The cutting-open branch lets the aperture function as release rather than defect alone.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity governs the quality of the utterance imagined as passing through the opened mouth.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B004: A second visible lip-cleft image makes the bodily activation recurrent rather than isolated.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Raised or announced sound supplies the voiced material moving through the aperture.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Remembrance flowing on the tongue supplies the purified speech released after the focus.

## fire prayer reversal
- reading: The focus can function as a reversal of contact: purification turns a trajectory of meeting consuming heat into one of remembered worship and guided following.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Opening supplies the turn out of one trajectory and into another.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Rectification supplies the reorientation that differentiates the two forms of contact.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B001: Meeting heat supplies the consuming contact immediately before the focus.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specific worship supplies the transformed contact immediately after the focus.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B007: The non-dominant following image recasts prayer as alignment behind a preceding guide rather than exposure to consuming heat.

## paired order
- reading: Purification rightly orders paired dimensions: hidden with manifest, mortal life with true benefit, and the near horizon with the later one.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Durable success supplies the criterion by which paired horizons are ordered.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B005: The pair-and-even branch supplies the unusual structural lens directly from the focus verb.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Manifest visibility supplies one member of an epistemic pair.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the paired interior member.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of life supplies one existential pole.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as good supplies the opposed existential pole.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the immediate member of the temporal pair.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness supplies the temporally opposed member.

## scriptural field
- reading: The individual is a fresh growth of an old textual cultivation whose pattern is replanted across successive records and recipients.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The tiller supplies cultivation as repeated work on a receptive surface.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth supplies the recurring yield produced in successive readers or communities.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Breadth and extension supply the receptive surface of the textual field.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet keeps the field explicitly textual and prevents the analogy from floating free.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply the inherited starting layer of cultivation.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome supplies recurrence in which an old inscription reaches a fresh realization.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant succession image supplies transmission from one instantiation to the next.


# Focus 87:15

## recollection to worship
- reading: Recovered awareness of the nurturing Lord is not left mental; ف turns it into a completed, embodied act of worship.
- mechanism: A held or recovered awareness is given a definite object by the Lord's name; the name identifies the one who nurtures and completes, and the following prayer converts that awareness into ordered bodily worship.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something to mind supplies the inward recovery that initiates the sequence.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: Name as raised designation gives recollection a definite, speakable point of orientation.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion make the named Lord the agent of sustained formation rather than a bare title.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the enacted form into which recollection passes.

## voiced elevation and appeal
- reading: Voicing the designation raises the Lord into acknowledged public presence, and prayer continues the same event as praise and appeal.
- mechanism: The Lord's designation is made to run on the tongue and thereby raised into public presence; prayer then answers that voiced elevation as praise, blessing, appeal, and reception of mercy.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue makes the first act audible rather than merely interior.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B007: Honorable mention and renown make the utterance an act of raising repute.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: Rising high supplies the vertical effect assigned to voicing the name.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and authority identify whose repute is raised and toward whom the response is directed.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B002: Supplication, praise, and mercy make prayer a responsive appeal rather than only a ritual label.

## name as legible mark
- reading: The name is also read as an identifying trace of formative lordship, and prayer is the body's displayed recognition of that trace.
- mechanism: The name functions not only as a label but as an identifying trace. Recollection recognizes that trace as evidence of the Lord's formative care, and prayer makes the recognition bodily legible.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible identifying mark turns the name into a trace by which belonging can be recognized.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B002: A sign read by discerning observation supplies the act of inference from mark to source.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing to mind supplies recognition of the identifying trace.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and completion identify the care inferred from the mark.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship becomes the visible response that makes recognition inspectable.

## opening command fulfilled
- reading: The focus performs the surah's opening command: the highest Lord's designation is voiced, then the commanded glorification becomes prayer.
- mechanism: The opening command to worshipfully declare the name of the highest Lord is answered in the focus by mentioning that same name and praying. The focus therefore reads as enactment and completion of the opening imperative.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Voiced mention supplies the focus act that answers the earlier command.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the bodily completion of that answer.
  - 87:1 **سَبِّحِ** س ب ح B001: Worship through glorification and prayer supplies the opening command's ritual scope.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: Name as raised designation repeats the focus object's exact functional identity.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and authority repeat the relational title that governs the focus response.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B001: Height and elevation give the opening naming act a vertical direction that the focus performs.

## created order alignment
- reading: Prayer places the body behind the measured, guiding, nurturing order carried by the Lord's name, including consent to created forms passing away.
- mechanism: The Lord named in the focus is characterized by measuring, straightening, planning, guiding, tending, and allowing growth to pass into debris. Prayer can therefore be read as embodied alignment with a governed order that includes both formation and dissolution.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion make lordship the continuous formative agency named in the focus.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B006: Following the leader in second place supplies a bodily image of alignment behind that agency.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supply the first operation in the created order.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion supply the ordered condition toward which formation moves.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Deliberate apportioning and planning make the order directed rather than accidental.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a way supplies movement through the measured order.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B002: Shepherding care and protection materialize the nurturing dimension of lordship.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming brittle debris prevents alignment from meaning attachment to one stable created form.

## recitation memory relay
- reading: The worshipper is the receptive end of a recitation-reminder circuit, and prayer is memory successfully carried into action.
- mechanism: The focus recollection becomes the downstream completion of a relay: recitation is imparted, forgetting is checked, a reminder is issued, benefit is tested, and an awed recipient recollects. Prayer is enacted retention, not an isolated mental event.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing a thing to mind supplies the focus endpoint of the mnemonic relay.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship turns retained content into durable performance.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and causing recitation supply the transmitted content at the relay's source.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance supplies the failure that the promised recitation prevents.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder and admonition convert retained recitation into a stimulus passed onward.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as the opposite of harm supplies the relay's functional test.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies the receptive condition under which reminder becomes self-recollection.

## public trace hidden integrity
- reading: Mention and prayer are the public trace of devotion, while their hidden orientation remains known beyond what observers can infer.
- mechanism: Voiced mention and visible prayer make orientation publicly legible, but the manifest trace does not exhaust the act. The contrast between what is announced and what is hidden places the external sign under a knower who sees both.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue supplies the audible exterior of remembrance.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B002: A sign read by discerning observation makes the name-prayer sequence socially legible.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the visible enacted trace.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure of a thing to a knower supplies knowledge that is not limited to public display.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Announcement and raised voice define the manifest side of the act.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment and hiddenness preserve an inward side not readable from the mark alone.

## eased kinetic following
- reading: Remembering catches an already facilitated direction, and prayer is the worshipper's light movement into line behind it.
- mechanism: Prayer's follower image is activated by divine facilitation toward ease. Remembering the Lord's name reorients the worshipper, while prayer is the light, compliant motion made possible along the eased way.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship supplies the authority and direction behind which the worshipper moves.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B006: The second racer following at the leader's flank supplies prayer's kinetic alignment.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty make the path available rather than self-produced.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Lightness and compliance in motion give the enabled path a bodily quality.

## reminder response bifurcation
- reading: The focus shows the receptive outcome of a live fork: one lets reminder become worship, while another shifts it aside and enters hardship.
- mechanism: The same reminder divides recipients: awe permits useful recollection, while the most burdened moves it to the side. The focus depicts one realized branch of reception, where recollection crosses into worship.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Recovered presence supplies the receptive branch realized in the focus.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship makes reception consequential rather than merely cognitive.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder that restores presence supplies the common stimulus offered to both paths.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit opposed to harm defines successful uptake of the reminder.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear with awe supplies the openness through which reminder becomes recollection.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Moving aside, distancing, and estrangement supply the competing response.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Hardship and burdensome suffering characterize the path that evades the reminder.

## prayer fire reversal
- reading: Prayer becomes the constructive orientation of a lexical field whose contextual alternative is being made to encounter the greatest fire.
- mechanism: The neighboring ṣ-l-w/y inventories hold both prescribed prayer and exposure to fire. Against the one who encounters the greatest fire, the focus presents one who directs the same sound-and-branch field toward worship, praise, and mercy.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the focus pole of the lexical reversal.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: The focus split inventory's fire encounter keeps the opposed pole latent inside the focus form.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Encountering fire and its heat makes the latent opposed pole explicit in context.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire and branding by it materialize the destructive encounter.
  - 87:12 **ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ** ك ب ر B001: Magnitude opposed to smallness intensifies the contextual fire pole.

## cultivated flourishing
- reading: Success is cultivated: purification opens and clears, the Lord's remembered nurture directs growth, and prayer emerges as its visible living result.
- mechanism: Flourishing is activated as both the farmer's opening of soil and durable success, while purification is also growth. Remembering the nurturing Lord and praying become stages in cultivation: clearing, orienting, and producing a visible living act.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B002: Nurture and stage-by-stage completion supply the cultivation carried by the Lord's title.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Recovered presence supplies the orientation of the cultivated subject.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies the visible act produced by the cultivation sequence.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The farmer cutting open the earth supplies the material operation behind flourishing.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Attainment and lasting survival supply the result sought by cultivation.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase make purification productive rather than merely subtractive.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness supply the clearing operation that permits growth.

## preference reversal
- reading: Prayer is a performed preference: remembrance breaks the monopoly of the near and chooses the deferred, better, enduring horizon.
- mechanism: The post-focus contrast exposes a rival selection rule: immediate life wins because it is near, whereas the later good endures. Remembering the Lord's name changes the scale of salience, and prayer performs preference for what is deferred but lasting.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing the Lord to mind counters the perceptual dominance of what is merely near.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship embodies the revised preference rather than leaving it an opinion.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Choosing one thing over another supplies the explicit selection operation.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good explains the genuine attraction of the selected object.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the salience bias that makes immediate life prevail.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the temporal disadvantage of the competing object.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B003: Seeking good through choice supplies a corrected criterion of selection.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance and remaining supply the horizon that outweighs immediate nearness.

## inherited record enactment
- reading: The worshipper activates an older binding record: naming acknowledges its Lord, and prayer performs the inherited claim in the present.
- mechanism: The focus branch of remembrance as a rights document or legal instrument is activated by the written former sheets. Mentioning the Lord's name and praying can therefore be carried as enactment of an inherited covenantal record, not invention at the moment of devotion.
- trace:
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B008: A document of right turns remembrance from mental recall toward an inspectable inherited instrument.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B011: A covenant or binding pact supplies the relational force carried by that instrument.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Prescribed worship supplies performance of the record rather than mere archival possession.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies the physical medium that activates remembrance's documentary branch.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet image ties the teaching to named earlier carriers.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and temporal precedence place the record before the present performance.

## rain marked growth
- reading: The Lord's name falls like a rain-mark that makes inward cultivation visible as prayer-growth, even while the pasture cycle warns that visible forms can become debris.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B003: First rain marking earth with vegetation lets the name act as a life-bearing signature.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B008: A low persistent cloud that nurtures plants supplies the named Lord's ecological agency.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B009: The grazing plant branch lets prayer appear, cautiously, as visible growth under that agency.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing supply the living field in which the material analogy operates.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry debris makes the devotional growth fragile rather than triumphally permanent.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase reconnect purification immediately before the focus to the vegetal field.

## follower at the haunch
- reading: Prayer also images taking the nearest following position behind the authority just named, moving where guidance has already opened the way.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B001: Lordship and authority identify the one whose lead orders the movement.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B006: The second racer at the leader's flank supplies the precise spatial image of close following.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B003: The guide who advances at the front supplies a contextual leader for the focus follower image.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Light compliant motion explains how the follower stays close rather than struggling independently.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: One thing following another supplies a second packet-supported image of ordered succession.

## fire straightening discipline
- reading: Prayer continues purification as a chosen formative exposure that straightens the self, reversing the contextual fire from destructive encounter into discipline.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B004: Kindling and fire-straightening supply the focus's latent formative heat image.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Straightness and completion supply the shape toward which disciplined formation moves.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B004: Fire used to straighten makes the material operation explicit beside the punitive fire scene.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness direct the heat image toward formation rather than destruction.


# Focus 87:16

## priority of near life
- reading: You actively install the nearer and lower life as the first-ranked benefit.
- mechanism: The line combines deliberate preference with a proximity-ranked object, so it describes an active ordering operation rather than passive fondness.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting something first supplies the deliberate rank-changing operation.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preferring one thing supplies the ordinary choice relation anchoring the model.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit or saving good makes the object an experienced field of value, not bare duration.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B002: The nearer, lower, or first side supplies the criterion by which this life is promoted.

## exclusive appropriation
- reading: The verse can also diagnose an outward practice of privatizing the benefit that lies nearest.
- mechanism: Preference can coexist with appropriation: the plural subjects do not only esteem immediate life but treat its accessible benefit as something to secure for themselves.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B006: Exclusive possession for oneself turns valuation into a mode of allocation.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit supplies what is being appropriated.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness makes that benefit immediately reachable and therefore easy to reserve.

## practiced trace following
- reading: The preference can be an acquired groove that repeatedly follows the most familiar nearby signs of life.
- mechanism: The ongoing plural choice may be a learned track: familiar near life leaves the clearest route, and repeated following turns proximity into practiced competence.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B004: Following a predecessor's trace supplies the path-dependent shape of the preference.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B011: Skill acquired by practice supplies repetition and habituation.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B003: Animate lived existence supplies the concrete track repeatedly navigated.
