  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness explains why this track is encountered and rehearsed first.

## vertical priority inversion
- reading: The lower-nearer pole is actively raised over the highest pole, making the preference an inversion of the window's vertical order.
- mechanism: The opening superlative height and the focus superlative nearness form opposed poles. Preference now appears as a vertical inversion that puts the lower-nearer pole first.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting first supplies the act that reverses the rank order.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B002: The nearer, lower, or first side supplies the promoted pole.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B001: Elevation supplies the opposite spatial pole against which worldly nearness is ranked.
  - 87:1 **ٱلْأَعْلَى** ع ل و B005: The upper direction makes the contrast a directional ordering, not only an abstract value judgment.

## proximity replaces calibration
- reading: The subjects use immediacy as a faulty metric, overriding a world already presented as measured and guided.
- mechanism: Creation is presented through measuring, balancing, planning, and guiding; the focus subjects replace that calibrated decision system with proximity as their practical metric.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference supplies the human selection operation being recalibrated.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the substitute heuristic used by the subjects.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning a thing supplies an objective scale beyond immediate proximity.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B006: Middle, equity, and an impartial place supply balanced evaluation.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Planning by measure and consideration supplies purposive calibration.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a way or truth supplies the offered decision path.

## surface phase of life cycle
- reading: Worldly life is a moving material cycle, and the error is selecting its nearest surface phase as though it were the whole.
- mechanism: The pasture sequence gives worldly life a material time course: living growth is made into dark flood-borne debris. Preference selects the nearby visible phase and mistakes a transient surface or residue for stable life.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B002: Earth made alive by rain and growth lets life be read as a vegetative process.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign of what was supplies the residue that can be mistaken for the whole process.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness explains why one currently visible phase dominates perception.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture and grazing supply the living productive phase.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: Making a thing become a state supplies the transformation arrow between phases.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Scum floating and carried by floodwater supplies the unstable surface endpoint.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming brittle debris explicitly joins living growth to its spent remainder.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Blackness or dark redness supplies the visual terminal state.

## memory visibility selection
- reading: The preference is partly an availability effect: the near-visible life crowds out value that must be carried by recitation, trace, and reminder.
- mechanism: Recitation and reminder preserve transmissible traces against forgetting, while overt and hidden form unequal visibility channels. The near life wins by availability unless reminder makes the non-near cognitively present.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B002: A report transmitted until handed down supplies durable counter-memory.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign supplies evidence that persists when its source is absent.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the availability advantage of immediate life.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, reciting, and causing recitation supply the preservation channel.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of remembrance supplies the failure mode that would erase distant value.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: What appears plainly to the eye supplies the high-salience channel.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the low-salience channel that preference can neglect.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back to mind supplies the corrective operation.

## easy groove against facilitated ease
- reading: The focus exposes a contest between two kinds of ease: inherited worldly fluency and an opened route that can retrain it.
- mechanism: The focus's practiced nearby track gains a contextually supplied friction profile. One ease is the familiar path of immediate life; another ease is deliberately opened toward al-yusra, so ease itself does not settle which path is sound.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B004: Following an existing trace supplies a preformed route.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B011: Practice-based skill supplies the route's learned fluency.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness makes the worldly route low-cost to enter.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supplies a competing facilitated route.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B005: Lightness and compliant movement supply the felt fluency of that alternative.

## preference as approach avoidance
- reading: Worldly life becomes dominant through both attraction to nearness and active removal of the reminder from the decision field.
- mechanism: Selecting the near object is paired with moving the reminder to the side. Preference therefore has two coordinated vectors, approach toward accessible life and active distancing from the disruptive alternative.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference supplies the approach vector toward one selected object.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the spatially favored destination.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Putting something at a distance or to the side supplies the avoidance vector.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B002: Hardship and suffering supply the aversive identity attached to that avoidance.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness as the opposite of felicity supplies the resulting condition.

## near life terminates in anti life
- reading: They choose the immediately vivid form of life in a sequence that can end by stripping life of benefit without granting death.
- mechanism: The context breaks the simple life-death binary by presenting a fire-state that is neither death nor life. Choosing near life can therefore culminate not in another kind of vitality but in deprivation of life as benefit.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and saving good supplies the qualitative meaning put at risk.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B002: The nearer life supplies the initially favored horizon.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Meeting fire and its heat supplies the enclosing consequence-state.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the material force of that state.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: Loss of strength and life supplies one explicitly negated endpoint.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit supplies the other explicitly negated endpoint, yielding anti-life rather than mere survival.

## rival enacted priority sequences
- reading: Preference is itself a counter-practice that enacts a rival hierarchy after purification, naming, and worship have enacted another.
- mechanism: Success, purification, mentioning the Name, and worship form an enacted sequence immediately before bal. The focus introduces a rival enacted sequence whose first operation is assigning priority to near life.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting something first supplies the first operation of the rival sequence.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference supplies the choice that enacts rather than merely states the hierarchy.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Attaining success and continuance supplies the outcome of the preceding sequence.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude supply its first self-directed practice.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue supplies the sequence's voiced act.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B005: The name as proclamation and signification supplies what the voiced act makes present.
  - 87:15 **رَبِّهِۦ** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and completing supply the relation acknowledged by the Name.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: The specific act of worship supplies the sequence's embodied completion.

## temporal discount and reserve
- reading: They apply a short horizon that overprices accessible benefit, while the next line values a delayed good precisely by its capacity to remain.
- mechanism: The adjacent comparison decomposes choice into horizon, value, and durability. Immediate appropriation discounts delayed good and consumes what a longer horizon would preserve as a lasting reserve.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting first supplies explicit priority assignment.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B006: Taking exclusively for oneself supplies immediate appropriation.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the short decision horizon.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit supplies the payoff being compared.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the longer horizon.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B003: Seeking good through choice makes the comparison an evaluative selection problem.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance and remaining supply durability.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B004: Holding part back and storing it supplies the reserve model opposed to immediate taking.

## inherited precedence corrects present priority
- reading: Its present priority is challenged by an older transmitted priority whose first records already direct attention to the later outcome.
- mechanism: The focus verb joins priority and transmitted trace, while the closing lines locate the comparison in first written records. An older transmission thus carries a future-oriented ranking that corrects the present act of putting nearness first.
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: Putting something first supplies present evaluative precedence.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B002: Transmitting a report until it is handed down supplies inherited continuity.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: A written sheet supplies the durable medium of transmission.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet image reinforces transmission across named predecessors.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence supply the antiquity of the corrective ranking.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to an outcome supplies the future end already encoded in an earlier record.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B008: The split-map image of priority and entitlement makes firstness echo a claim to rightful precedence.

## nearest visible mark as false destination
- reading: The subjects may be trackers who mistake the nearest visible mark of life for the destination to which the whole sign system points.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B008: Marking a hoof so its track can be followed supplies a concrete sign-following mechanism.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign pointing to what was lets a visible mark stand for an absent source.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness biases the tracker toward the first legible mark.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible mark that makes a thing known supplies a split-map sign parallel to the focus trace.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B002: A distinguishing mark that guides toward a thing supplies the navigational function of signs.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Plain ocular appearance supplies the tempting visible channel.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Hiddenness supplies the destination that cannot be inferred from immediate visibility alone.

## stirred surface recruits preference
- reading: Near life may recruit preference by erupting into the perceptual foreground, so salience stirs the chooser before the chooser ranks it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B001: A thing bursting up and spreading outward supplies sudden visible salience.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B002: Stirring something from its settled state supplies the activation event that recruits attention.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B002: Rain-driven growth supplies a life process capable of visible eruption.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness makes the stirred event perceptually dominant.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Scum rising and being carried on water supplies a context image of unstable surface salience.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark coloration supplies the visible state that remains after the stirring cycle.

## cultivation versus appropriation
- reading: The subjects seize the nearest yield as private product instead of inhabiting life as cultivation, increase, and preserved remainder.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B006: Exclusive taking for oneself supplies appropriation of the current yield.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B002: Earth brought alive by rain and growth supplies the productive field.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the temptation to take the present yield.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The farmer splitting the earth supplies labor that opens a future yield.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the cultivated process rather than the seized product.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B004: Holding something back and storing it supplies preservation beyond immediate consumption.


# Focus 87:17

## qualified endurance
- reading: The latter horizon is preferred because its benefit and its resistance to extinction coincide, while remaining analytically distinct.
- mechanism: Laterness supplies the compared horizon, goodness supplies the reason for preference, and persistence supplies resistance to loss. Keeping the last two predicates distinct prevents mere longevity from counting automatically as good.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The later-or-other-than-first branch establishes the relational horizon being compared.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: The desired and beneficial good branch supplies qualitative superiority, not just temporal succession.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: The persistence-against-perishing branch supplies the durability dimension of the comparison.

## deferred reserve
- reading: The verse can also stage a choice between immediate expenditure and a better deferred reserve.
- mechanism: The focus can stage an allocation decision: value not exhausted in the first horizon is deferred, selected as the better option, and conserved as reserve. Delay is therefore not emptiness but protected capacity.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: The deferral-to-a-later-time branch supplies postponed realization rather than simple absence.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B003: The choosing-the-better-option branch turns the comparison into a decision between alternatives.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B004: The held-back reserve branch makes durability function as conserved, unspent capacity.

## preserving beneficence
- reading: As a live secondary reading, the latter realm may also be more preserving: its goodness consists partly in what it spares from loss.
- mechanism: Alongside passive endurance, the final predicate can echo an agentive relation: the latter order gives more good by sparing, preserving, or refusing total loss. This remains a secondary activation rather than a replacement gloss.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The latter-or-other branch locates the proposed preserving order beyond the first one.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: The beneficence-and-gift branch renders goodness as something given to a recipient.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B003: The sparing-and-keeping-alive branch supplies active preservation instead of duration alone.

## decay quality filter
- reading: The latter is superior as good persistence, not mere persistence: the surah has already shown that some later residues are worthless.
- mechanism: The pasture-to-debris sequence provides a negative test for the baseline: a thing can be later and can remain as residue without being better. The separate predicate of goodness therefore filters persistence, distinguishing valuable survival from waste left by transformation.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: The beneficial-good branch becomes the quality filter that mere sequence and residue lack.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B002: The remaining-portion branch supplies residue as a neutral category whose value must still be tested.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: The pasture branch supplies a living and useful initial state for the context's material transformation.
  - 87:5 **فَجَعَلَهُۥ** ج ع ل B002: The making-something-into-a-state branch supplies the transition arrow from pasture to its later condition.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: The pasture-become-dry-debris branch supplies a later remainder that is durable enough to remain but not desirable.

## retrievable persistence
- reading: It can also describe good that remains retrievable and beneficial through recitation, memory, and renewed reminder.
- mechanism: Recitation, threatened forgetting, renewed recollection, and benefit form a retention circuit. This activates a cognitive and transmissive mode of persistence: a good remains not merely by existing somewhere, but by staying recoverable and effective for a hearer.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: The beneficial-good branch makes retained content count as good when it continues to produce benefit.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B002: The remaining-effect branch supplies persistence as an accessible remainder rather than bare elapsed duration.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: The reading-and-recitation branch supplies the repeated act that keeps content available.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: The loss-from-memory branch supplies the failure mode against which persistence is measured.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B003: The restoring-to-mind branch supplies recovery after possible forgetting and closes the retention circuit.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: The benefit-against-harm branch makes efficacy, not storage alone, the criterion of successful retention.

## hidden latter
- reading: The latter may also be the presently concealed other register: absent to sight, present to knowledge, and no less enduring.
- mechanism: The manifest-hidden pair places reality on an accessibility axis as well as a time axis. The latter or other horizon can therefore be presently absent from view yet fully within knowledge; its concealment need not weaken its comparative reality or persistence.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The later-or-other branch permits the compared horizon to be relationally absent rather than only chronologically future.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: The persistence branch lets the concealed horizon remain real despite lacking present visibility.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: The disclosure-to-a-knower branch supplies epistemic access that does not depend on public visibility.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: The visibly manifest branch supplies one pole of the context's accessibility contrast.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: The concealment branch supplies the other pole and activates an unseen register for the latter horizon.

## duration needs life
- reading: The praised endurance is enduring beneficial life; the passage explicitly permits a terrible continuation that is neither death nor genuine life.
- mechanism: The fiery state in which one neither dies nor lives is a negative control for endurance. It can continue without qualifying as beneficial life, so the focus's two predicates must work together: what is more lasting is preferable only as a mode of living good, not as endless continuation by itself.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: The beneficial-good branch excludes painful continuation from the kind of endurance praised by the focus.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: The persistence branch supplies duration, which the context then shows can be either good or dreadful.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: The encountering-fire-and-heat branch supplies the harmful environment of the counterexample.
  - 87:12 **ٱلنَّارَ** ن و ر B002: The kindled-fire branch makes the continuing condition materially active rather than neutral duration.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B001: The loss-of-life branch supplies one denied exit from the continuing state.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: The life-as-benefit-and-good branch makes the denial of life a denial of qualitative flourishing, not merely biological status.

## near choice reversal
- reading: The focus is an intervention in choice: do not equate the nearest benefit with the best one when the deferred good preserves more value.
- mechanism: Preference for the near life exposes a salience bias: proximity is being mistaken for value. The focus answers with a three-step correction, moving from the near object of choice to the deferred alternative, then naming it the better option and the conserved reserve.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: The deferred-time branch supplies the option discounted because it is not immediately available.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B003: The choosing-the-better branch turns the focus into a corrective decision rule.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B004: The reserve branch supplies value conserved beyond immediate use.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: The life-as-benefit branch supplies the genuinely attractive good whose near form competes for preference.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: The preference-by-choice branch supplies the human selection that the focus immediately reevaluates.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: The nearness branch identifies proximity, rather than badness in life itself, as the feature distorting selection.

## first later transmission
- reading: The judgment favoring the latter is itself ancient and repeatedly transmitted: laterness concerns the valued horizon, not novelty of the teaching.
- mechanism: The claim that privileges the latter is located in the first written records. First and latter therefore form a transmission loop rather than a simple replacement: an early inscription carries an outcome-oriented judgment through successive records, making the later-directed teaching itself a surviving remainder.
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: The later-than-first branch supplies the horizon praised by a teaching already present at the opposite temporal pole.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B002: The surviving-remainder branch supplies the teaching's persistence across records and receptions.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet branch supplies an archival medium in which the later-directed judgment precedes its present recitation.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet branch extends the archival claim across separately named prior records.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: The beginning-and-precedence branch supplies the first pole that unexpectedly carries praise of the latter.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The return-to-outcome branch folds beginning toward final consequence and makes the temporal poles mutually legible.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: The non-dominant succession branch supplies record-after-record continuity without collapsing the packet's split mapping.

## rear choice residue
- reading: As a contained material analogy, the rear of a process reveals its remainder, and goodness distinguishes the choice residue from discard.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B003: The physical-rear branch turns the latter horizon into the back end of a material process.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B002: The chosen-excellence branch marks one terminal product as selected rather than incidental.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B002: The residue branch supplies what is left after the process has run.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B004: The negligible-debris branch supplies the rejected end-product against which a choice remainder is distinguished.

## preserving order
- reading: As a secondary causative echo, the latter may also preserve better: its beneficence repairs, spares, and keeps faith with what would otherwise be lost.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B005: The beneficence-and-gift branch makes the latter good relationally generous.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B003: The sparing-and-keeping-alive branch supplies the exploratory agentive force of preserving another.
  - 87:1 **رَبِّكَ** ر ب ب B002: The nurturing, repairing, and completing branch supplies an agent who maintains value toward completion.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B006: The preserving-and-covenant-care branch gives active maintenance a social and fiduciary shape.

## rerouted reserve
- reading: As a contained spatial analogy, blocking the nearest outlet can reroute desire toward a later opening where value exits without exhausting the reserve.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: The deferral branch supplies the other opening as a later route rather than a vanished good.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B006: The coaxing-out-through-another-opening branch supplies the unusual redirection mechanism.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B004: The held-reserve branch makes what is denied immediate release remain available through the alternate route.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B001: The movement-to-an-exterior branch supplies the actual exit phase of the spatial analogy.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: The preference branch supplies desire moving toward one opening rather than another.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: The nearness branch identifies the immediately available opening that captures preference.


# Focus 87:18

## prior textual attestation
- reading: This teaching is emphatically attested as content within written predecessors.
- mechanism: A salient but internally unspecified 'this' is not merely old wisdom; it is presented as recoverable textual content already borne by earlier written witnesses.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet image supplies the concrete textual container asserted by في.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence place those written witnesses before the present utterance in time or rank.

## successive archive
- reading: The first sheets form a successive, internally plural archive of witness.
- mechanism: Collected sheets and uninterrupted succession form a relay: priorness belongs to a sequence of textual witnesses whose continuity matters as much as their age.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: The collected-sheets image turns the plural into an archive assembled from multiple textual units.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Continuous succession supplies the ordering relation that links the prior sheets into a relay.

## origin bearing outcome
- reading: The textual beginning is also a source to which the teaching's eventual meaning returns.
- mechanism: The first sheets can function as an origin that already bears a later meaning: textual precedence and eventual interpretation coexist rather than pointing in opposite directions.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Written sheets anchor the reading in actual textual predecessors rather than free temporal symbolism.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome or final meaning makes the earliest textual point capable of carrying its later interpretive destination.

## created world as legible sheet
- reading: Alongside manuscript attestation, the created world's measured and decaying surface can act as a primordial legible sheet displaying the same teaching.
- mechanism: The opening sequence describes marking, measured formation, ordered surfaces, guidance, emergence, and darkened vegetal residue. Against the broad-surface branch of الصحف, the created world becomes a first legible substrate on which a recurrent order is displayed.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Flat spread and breadth supply the focus ayah's material surface onto which the contextual process can be read.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence let the displayed order count as a primordial sheet before later verbal inscription.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible mark that makes a thing known supplies the legibility relation between surface and meaning.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning make creation an articulated pattern rather than an unstructured backdrop.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B009: The broad smooth ground image materially links ordered creation to the spread surface of a sheet.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B001: A measured limit supplies repeatable boundaries within the world-pattern.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path makes the measured pattern semantically directive.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment turns latent order into a visible sign.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B002: Pasture becoming dry debris writes transformation and loss into the same displayed cycle.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Darkening supplies the final visible mark by which the completed cycle remains readable.

## archive to oral memory circuit
- reading: The teaching remains available through a written-oral-memory circuit that gathers, voices, preserves, conceals, and reactivates it.
- mechanism: Reading gathers written material into recitation; preservation against forgetting keeps it latent; disclosure across voiced and hidden states and repeated reminding returns it to use. Being 'in the sheets' therefore names durable availability within a cross-medium memory circuit, not inert storage.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet is the durable material pole from which recitation and recall can reactivate content.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Continuous succession lets oral and written states belong to one uninterrupted transmission.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B001: Gathering and collecting supply the operation by which dispersed textual matter becomes a recitable whole.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, recitation, and causing to recite move the archive into voiced performance.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss from memory names the failure that durable inscription and renewed recitation jointly resist.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure to a knower makes recovery of latent content, rather than mere possession, the epistemic event.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B001: Open declaration and raised voice supply the public state of transmitted content.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the latent state from which the same content may later be recovered.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty describe successful access to what the archive preserves.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder is the prompt that reactivates preserved content in a hearer.

## prior sheets as reception map
- reading: They also preserve a repeatable map of how the message is remembered or avoided and what each response produces.
- mechanism: The reminder produces opposed responses: reverent recall or active distancing, followed by opposed existential consequences. The deictic 'this' can therefore include a recurrent reception law already mapped in the earlier sheets, not only a doctrinal sentence.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Written sheets provide the prior record in which the response pattern is said to recur.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Precedence makes the recorded pattern older than its present enactment.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: The reminder supplies the common stimulus from which the two response paths diverge.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies receptive seriousness on the remembering path.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Distancing and withdrawal supply the contrary act of moving the reminder aside.
  - 87:11 **ٱلْأَشْقَى** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposed to flourishing characterizes the subject formed by refusal.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Encounter with fire supplies the consequence reached by the distancing path.
  - 87:13 **يَمُوتُ** م و ت B012: Stillness and dormancy sharpen the suspended condition without granting final release.
  - 87:13 **يَحْيَىٰ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good explains why continued existence can nevertheless fail to count as living.

## text to embodied succession
- reading: Continuity also occurs when a present person voices and embodies the pattern carried by the earlier sheets.
- mechanism: Flourishing, purification, voiced remembrance, and worship make the teaching effective as practice. The succession branch attached to الأولى then lets present enactment follow earlier sheets, so continuity is behavioral as well as textual.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet supplies the inherited textual pattern that practice can enact.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Continuous succession turns relation to earlier sheets into following without an intervening break.
  - 87:14 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success and continuance supply the realized outcome of receiving the inherited pattern.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude supply the self-transforming operation by which the pattern becomes lived.
  - 87:15 **وَذَكَرَ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention flowing on the tongue converts inherited content into present utterance.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the bodily completion of the textual and vocal sequence.

## firstness folded toward last
- reading: The first sheets matter because from the beginning they redirect choice toward the later and enduring outcome.
- mechanism: The immediate contrast opposes choosing near life to the later, better, enduring one. When followed by الأولى, chronological firstness folds toward outcome: the earliest sheets already direct choice away from the proximal and toward what lasts.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Written sheets anchor the temporal fold in inherited textual witness.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence establish the genuinely early pole of the fold.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Return to outcome or final meaning lets that early pole carry orientation toward an end.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference by choice supplies the decision whose temporal orientation the sheets evaluate.
  - 87:16 **ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ** ح ي ي B013: Life as benefit and good exposes the question of which apparent life actually deserves the name.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the proximal attraction set against the temporally later good.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B001: Laterness after a first supplies the remote temporal pole.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good assigns positive value to the later pole rather than to mere antiquity.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Duration and remaining supply the endpoint toward which the earliest witness points.

## trace following transmission
- reading: The current teaching follows, revoices, and preserves a surviving textual trace from earlier witnesses.
- mechanism: The nonlocal branches of تؤثرون activate transmitted report, surviving mark, and following a prior trace. Joined to enduring remainder and the repeated sheets in 87:19, the focus claim becomes an act of trace-following transmission rather than a bare assertion of similarity.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Written sheets provide the material traces that can survive and be followed.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: Continuous succession supplies the relay linking an earlier trace to a present utterance.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B002: Transmission of a report until it becomes received tradition supplies the communicative relay.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign that indicates what was supplies the evidentiary status of an old sheet.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B004: Walking after a prior trace supplies the reader-inferred direction from earlier witness to present proclamation.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B002: What remains of a thing supplies the durable residue that makes transmission across time possible.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet image confirms that the relay terminates in identifiable earlier textual witnesses.

## named plural witnesses
- reading: They are a plural, named chain of witnesses converging on the content designated by 'this.'
- mechanism: The next ayah repeats the sheet noun and distributes it across two coordinated named figures. That repetition resolves the focus plural as multiple situated witnesses while leaving open whether they form one corpus or parallel archives.
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Collected sheets permit the focus plural to be read as a corpus assembled from more than one witness.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Precedence preserves their status as earlier witnesses when the following ayah names them.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The repeated written-sheet image concretizes the previously generic archive at the point of named provenance.

## prior claim not only prior date
- reading: Their teaching also presses a prior and worthier claim upon the chooser.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Written sheets keep the outlier attached to inherited textual teaching.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B008: Priority and superior right add a normative claim to the sheets' chronological priority.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference by choice supplies the decision arena in which one claim is given priority.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Beneficial good supplies the criterion by which the inherited claim can be judged worthier.

## sheets as receptive basins
- reading: As a contained material analogy, they are receptive basins in which the residue of prior cycles settles into transmissible form.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B004: Broad shallow basins supply receptive vessels in which prior material can collect.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B005: Thickening or coagulating at a process's end supplies the sediment-like stabilization of what a vessel holds.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating matter carried by a torrent supplies the residue available to be caught and retained.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B008: Hollows and flats that retain water supply the contextual collecting structure parallel to a basin.

## first rain inscription
- reading: As an explicit analogy, they are a first marking rain whose pattern can be followed by renewed recitation and fresh growth.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Flat spread and breadth supply the land-like receptive surface of the analogy.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B010: Rain following the wasmi supplies a sequence in which a later shower renews an earlier marking.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B003: The first rain marking earth with vegetation supplies the initial inscription in the rain analogy.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the visible growth produced on the marked receptive surface.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase transfer the rain sequence into the human reception of renewed teaching.


# Focus 87:19

## named written witnesses
- reading: Plural written witnesses whose authority remains indexed to two distinguishable prophetic provenances.
- mechanism: The written-sheet branch makes the phrase a pair of provenance-bearing textual corpora. The proper names do more than identify a topic: they keep two lines of witness visible inside one compact attribution.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet image supplies the textual medium, while the two following names organize that medium as attributed witness.

## open spread surfaces
- reading: Two traditions laid out as broad, inspectable surfaces of disclosure.
- mechanism: Flat spread and breadth foreground the sheet as an exposed surface. The phrase can therefore evoke teachings laid open side by side, with Abraham and Moses occupying a shared plane of disclosure without being collapsed into one source.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Flat breadth turns the named sheets from closed containers into surfaces on which distinct testimony can be openly spread.

## collectable plural archive
- reading: A gatherable constellation of leaves in which distinct Abrahamic and Mosaic provenances remain legible.
- mechanism: The codex branch pulls separate leaves toward an assembled archive, but the syntax does not require one physical volume. A live reading is a collectable cross-prophetic dossier whose unity consists in juxtaposition rather than source erasure.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Collected sheets supply an archival gathering mechanism, while the coordinated names preserve two components within the gathering.

## preservation with graphic risk
- reading: The named sheets preserve a trace while remaining exposed to specifically visual transmission error.
- mechanism: Writing stabilizes a trace but introduces a medium-specific vulnerability: what is preserved can be misread. The two attributions keep provenance visible, yet the focus alone does not show whether parallel provenance corrects or compounds transmission error.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet image supplies durable inscription as the condition for preservation.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B005: Misreading from similar written forms supplies the graphic failure mode carried by that same preserving medium.

## names as provenance marks
- reading: Abraham and Moses function as visible provenance marks by which two bodies of sheets remain recognizable.
- mechanism: The non-dominant visible-mark branch activated by the repeated word for name makes the two proper names in the focus behave like legible marks on sheet-clusters. Attribution becomes part of the artifact's recognizability, not merely external ownership.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet provides the carrier on which an identifying provenance can be imagined as visibly registered.
  - 87:1 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: A visible mark that makes something known turns the opening occurrence of name into an activator for marked provenance.
  - 87:15 **ٱسْمَ** س م و B001: The repeated identifying-mark image near the close reinforces naming as a recognition mechanism before the final named sheets.

## formed measured guidance surface
- reading: Deliberately formed and proportioned guidance-surfaces whose textual shape participates in directing the reader.
- mechanism: The created-leveled and measured-guided sequences recast the broad written surface as a formed interface: its arrangement, proportion, and directive use belong together. The sheets become patterned guidance rather than containers accidentally holding guidance.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B001: Flat breadth supplies the surface whose formation and arrangement can carry directional force.
  - 87:2 **خَلَقَ** خ ل ق B001: Estimating and measuring a thing supplies deliberate formation rather than an accidental textual surface.
  - 87:2 **فَسَوَّىٰ** س و ي B002: Intrinsic straightness and completion make the formed surface fit for coherent presentation.
  - 87:3 **قَدَّرَ** ق د ر B005: Measured planning supplies the ordered disposition by which textual elements can serve a designed end.
  - 87:3 **فَهَدَىٰ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies the functional direction produced by the ordered surface.

## trace arrested from material flux
- reading: Selective trace-surfaces that resist the cycle by which exposed material becomes dark, mobile residue.
- mechanism: Emergence from concealment followed by floating dark residue creates a material cycle of disclosure and dispersal. Against that cycle, a written spread can be read as a surface that arrests selected traces before they are swept away as undifferentiated remainder.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet supplies a surface capable of retaining a selected trace through material change.
  - 87:4 **أَخْرَجَ** خ ر ج B002: Bringing something out of concealment supplies the first movement from latent content into exposure.
  - 87:5 **غُثَآءً** غ ث و B001: Floating debris borne by a torrent supplies the counter-image of traces dispersed without stable selection.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B006: Dark coloration marks the terminal altered state against which inscription appears as arrested visibility.

## oral written memory circuit
- reading: Active interfaces among inscription, recitation, memory, disclosure, and the possibility of visually induced error.
- mechanism: Recitation moves inscription into voice and memory; forgetting and the manifest-hidden pair make access reversible rather than automatic. The sheets become external memory traces that can recover what recitation makes present, while their own graphic form can also hide meaning through misreading.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written-sheet image supplies the external trace that can re-enter voiced recitation and memory.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B005: Graphic misreading supplies a second kind of concealment internal to the visible memory aid.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading, recitation, and causing to read supply the oral activation of the written trace.
  - 87:6 **تَنسَىٰٓ** ن س ي B001: Loss of recollection supplies the memory failure against which a recoverable trace has force.
  - 87:7 **يَعْلَمُ** ع ل م B001: Disclosure of a thing to a knower supplies the successful endpoint of the recitation-memory circuit.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Visible manifestation supplies the accessible face of inscription and voiced disclosure.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the latent side from which meaning must be recovered rather than merely seen.

## sheet as usable reminder
- reading: A reusable reminder-medium whose accessibility, benefit, and reception determine whether preserved teaching becomes active.
- mechanism: Ease, reminder, benefit, and receptive awe turn textual survival into use. The named sheets are not valuable merely because they endure; they are carriers whose content becomes effective when opened into accessible recollection and answered by a responsive reader.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheet supplies the durable carrier from which a reminder can be repeatedly made present.
  - 87:8 **وَنُيَسِّرُكَ لِلْيُسْرَىٰ** ي س ر B001: Opening and ease after difficulty supply accessibility as a condition for the sheet's use.
  - 87:9 **فَذَكِّرْ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder-object supplies the operational function that transforms stored writing into renewed recollection.
  - 87:9 **نَّفَعَتِ** ن ف ع B001: Benefit as the opposite of harm supplies a criterion of effect rather than mere textual presence.
  - 87:10 **يَخْشَىٰ** خ ش ي B001: Fear joined with awe supplies the receptive posture through which the reminder becomes consequential.

## reception forks into prayer or fire
- reading: The sheets are a decision surface whose remembered content can be embodied as either distancing toward fire or purification toward prayer.
- mechanism: The same remembered teaching can become a decision surface. One trajectory moves aside and encounters fire; the other purifies and becomes prayer. The split but overlapping fire-prayer branch inventories compress two opposed embodiments around one textual reminder.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The written sheets supply the common reminder-medium from which opposed modes of reception can diverge.
  - 87:11 **وَيَتَجَنَّبُهَا** ج ن ب B003: Withdrawal and distancing supply the first trajectory, a deliberate movement away from the reminder.
  - 87:12 **يَصْلَى** ص ل ي B003: Meeting the heat of fire supplies the embodied endpoint of the distancing trajectory.
  - 87:14 **تَزَكَّىٰ** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectification supply the contrary trajectory of receptive transformation.
  - 87:15 **فَصَلَّىٰ** ص ل و B003: Specified worship supplies the embodied endpoint of the receptive trajectory.

## durable countertrace to near choice
- reading: Their very survival is a countertrace that makes longer-lasting value present against the pull of what is merely near.
- mechanism: The context contrasts chosen nearness with delayed, beneficial endurance. Within the preference root's inventory, choosing and a remaining trace coexist; the named sheets become precisely such a surviving trace, present evidence that interrupts short-horizon preference.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Collected sheets supply a surviving archive able to carry a counterclaim across temporal distance.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B005: Preference by choice supplies the decision that the surviving sheets confront.
  - 87:16 **تُؤْثِرُونَ** ء ث ر B003: A remaining sign of what preceded supplies the trace mechanism by which ancient sheets can still act in the present.
  - 87:16 **ٱلدُّنْيَا** د ن و B001: Nearness supplies the short temporal horizon favored by immediate choice.
  - 87:17 **وَٱلْءَاخِرَةُ** ء خ ر B002: Deferral to a later time supplies the horizon that the sheets make imaginatively present.
  - 87:17 **خَيْرٌ** خ ي ر B001: Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the positive value carried across that longer horizon.
  - 87:17 **وَأَبْقَىٰٓ** ب ق ي B001: Continuance and remaining supply the durability that lets the textual trace outlast immediate preference.

## former sheets as provenance relay
- reading: A provenance expansion of the preceding claim and a relay node in which earlier named witnesses remain successively active.
- mechanism: The repeated sheet noun in 87:18 makes the focus an expansion of provenance rather than an isolated fragment. Precedence supplies the earlier station, while the non-dominant succession branch keeps Abraham and Moses as relay points in a sequence of witness rather than two sealed antiquities.
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The focus occurrence supplies the named written corpora that resolve the prior reference to sheets.
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B003: Collected leaves permit distinct witnesses to be held in one relay without asserting one original physical codex.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The immediately repeated written-sheet image creates the antecedent that 87:19 specifies by name.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B001: Beginning and precedence locate the named sheets at an earlier station of the textual witness.
  - 87:18 **ٱلْأُولَىٰ** ء و ل B002: One thing following another supplies the exploratory relay mechanism connecting distinct stations of witness.

## shallow basin of teaching
- reading: The named traditions are shallow receptive surfaces that gather, hold, and make available what would otherwise pass over them.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B004: A broad shallow receptacle supplies the odd material model of a tradition that gathers and offers what it receives.
  - 87:4 **ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ** ر ع ي B001: Pasture supplies the life-bearing material whose emergence makes receptivity ecologically consequential.
  - 87:5 **أَحْوَىٰ** ح و ي B008: Excavated flats that retain water supply a contextual analogue for shallow textual surfaces that gather rather than shed.

## dual witness error check
- reading: Visible writing can itself conceal through graphic confusion, while the Abraham-Moses pairing may offer two provenance lines for cautious comparison.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B005: Error caused by confused written forms supplies the transmission problem to which dual provenance could respond.
  - 87:6 **سَنُقْرِئُكَ** ق ر ء B002: Reading and causing to read supply the event in which a graphic ambiguity becomes consequential.
  - 87:7 **ٱلْجَهْرَ** ج ه ر B002: Manifest visibility supplies what seems available for verification on the sheet.
  - 87:7 **يَخْفَىٰ** خ ف ي B001: Concealment supplies the possibility that a visibly present form still hides or distorts its intended reading.

## beginning folded into outcome
- reading: Secondarily, their beginning folds toward an outcome: the Abrahamic and Mosaic witness returns as a still-active present claim.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 87:19 **صُحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: The named written sheets anchor the temporal resonance in a concrete textual witness.
  - 87:18 **ٱلصُّحُفِ** ص ح ف B002: Immediate repetition identifies the focus sheets with the contextual location in which the temporal branch is activated.
